    });
    expect(resolver.contextBeforeDispatch).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
    expect(resolver.contextAfterDispatch).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    expect(resolver.contextBeforeDispatch.mock.invocationCallOrder[0]).toBeLessThan(resolver.contextAfterDispatch.mock.invocationCallOrder[0]);
    expect(acceptance.accept).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('gives a detected context retirement during diagnostic persistence precedence with no external-id write', async () => {
    const { service, resolver, acceptance } = setup({ outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'ACK_WITHOUT_ID', legacyCode: 'SEND_RESULT_UNAVAILABLE' });
    acceptance.recordAmbiguousOutbound.mockImplementation(async () => {
      resolver.contextAfterDispatch.mockResolvedValue('CHANGED');
      throw new Error('identity fence rejected diagnostic transaction');
    });
    await expect(service.send(input())).rejects.toMatchObject({
      status: 409, response: { code: 'CHANNEL_CONTEXT_CHANGED', outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'ACK_WITHOUT_ID', diagnosticPersistence: 'UNCONFIRMED' },
    });
    expect(acceptance.recordAmbiguousOutbound).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(acceptance.accept).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('reports a confirmed diagnostic write as RECORDED if the identity retires immediately afterward', async () => {
    const { service, resolver, acceptance } = setup({ outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'ACK_WITHOUT_ID', legacyCode: 'SEND_RESULT_UNAVAILABLE' });
    resolver.contextAfterDispatch.mockResolvedValueOnce('CURRENT').mockResolvedValueOnce('CHANGED');
    await expect(service.send(input())).rejects.toMatchObject({
      status: 409, response: { code: 'CHANNEL_CONTEXT_CHANGED', outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'ACK_WITHOUT_ID', diagnosticPersistence: 'RECORDED' },
    });
    expect(acceptance.recordAmbiguousOutbound).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('does not start Message acceptance when the identity changed after an acknowledged send', async () => {
    const { service, resolver, acceptance, prisma } = setup({ outcome: 'ACCEPTED', externalMessageId: 'external-1' });
    resolver.contextAfterDispatch.mockResolvedValue('CHANGED');
    prisma.message.findUnique.mockResolvedValue(null);
    await expect(service.send(input())).rejects.toMatchObject({
      status: 409, response: { code: 'CHANNEL_CONTEXT_CHANGED', outcome: 'ACCEPTED_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE', localMessageState: 'UNCONFIRMED' },
    });
    expect(acceptance.accept).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('reports uncertainty before local Message acceptance as UNCONFIRMED', async () => {
    const { service, resolver, acceptance, prisma } = setup({ outcome: 'ACCEPTED', externalMessageId: 'external-1' });
    acceptance.accept.mockRejectedValue(new Error('fixture transaction did not confirm'));
    resolver.contextAfterDispatch.mockResolvedValue('CURRENT');
    prisma.message.findUnique.mockResolvedValue(null);
    await expect(service.send(input())).rejects.toMatchObject({
      status: 503, response: { code: 'OUTBOUND_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE', outcome: 'ACCEPTED_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE', localMessageState: 'UNCONFIRMED' },
    });
  });

  it('reports a confirmed Message commit as RECORDED after a later local finalization failure', async () => {
    const { service, acceptance, prisma, tx } = setup({ outcome: 'ACCEPTED', externalMessageId: 'external-1' });
    prisma.$transaction.mockImplementationOnce(async (callback: (tx: unknown) => unknown) => callback(tx));
    prisma.$transaction.mockRejectedValue(new Error('fixture conversation finalization failure'));
    await expect(service.send(input())).rejects.toMatchObject({
      status: 503, response: { code: 'OUTBOUND_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE', outcome: 'ACCEPTED_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE', localMessageState: 'RECORDED' },
    });
    expect(acceptance.accept).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('does not report complete ordinary success when the durable settlement is explicitly unconfirmed', async () => {
    const { service, acceptance, admissions, channel } = setup({ outcome: 'ACCEPTED', externalMessageId: 'external-1' });
    admissions.settleOrdinary.mockResolvedValue(false);
    await expect(service.send(input())).rejects.toMatchObject({
      status: 503, response: { code: 'OUTBOUND_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE', outcome: 'ACCEPTED_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE', localMessageState: 'RECORDED' },
    });
    expect(channel.sendMessage).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(acceptance.accept).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(admissions.settleOrdinary).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ id: 'admission-1', ownerNonce: 'captured-nonce', state: 'SETTLED' });
  });

  it('keeps a known committed Message RECORDED and returns context-changed after a local finalization failure', async () => {
    const { service, resolver, acceptance, prisma, tx } = setup({ outcome: 'ACCEPTED', externalMessageId: 'external-1' });
    prisma.$transaction.mockImplementationOnce(async (callback: (tx: unknown) => unknown) => callback(tx));
    prisma.$transaction.mockRejectedValue(new Error('fixture finalization failure'));
    resolver.contextAfterDispatch.mockResolvedValueOnce('CURRENT').mockResolvedValueOnce('CHANGED');
    await expect(service.send(input())).rejects.toMatchObject({
      status: 409, response: { code: 'CHANNEL_CONTEXT_CHANGED', outcome: 'ACCEPTED_LOCAL_INCOMPLETE', localMessageState: 'RECORDED' },
    });
    expect(acceptance.accept).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
});


## FULL SOURCE BODY api-app/src/integrations/instagram/instagram-messaging.client.spec.ts

import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { InstagramMessagingClient } from './instagram-messaging.client';

describe('InstagramMessagingClient acknowledgement boundary', () => {
  const response = (body: unknown) => ({ ok: true, json: async () => body }) as Response;

  it.each([
    ['ASCII', 'a'.repeat(1000)],
    ['accented', 'é'.repeat(500)],
    ['supplementary emoji', '🙂'.repeat(250)],
  ] as const)('sends the exact %s payload at 1000 UTF-8 bytes', async (_label, text) => {
    const request = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(response({ recipient_id: 'peer-1', message_id: 'mid-1' }));
    const beforeDispatch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(true);
    await expect(new InstagramMessagingClient(request).sendMessage('account-1', 'peer-1', text, 'token', beforeDispatch))
      .resolves.toEqual({ outcome: 'ACCEPTED', externalMessageId: 'mid-1' });
    expect(beforeDispatch).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
    expect(request).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
    expect(JSON.parse(String(request.mock.calls[0]?.[1]?.body))).toEqual({ recipient: { id: 'peer-1' }, message: { text } });
  });

  it.each([
    ['ASCII', 'a'.repeat(1001)],
    ['accented', 'é'.repeat(501)],
    ['supplementary emoji', '🙂'.repeat(251)],
  ] as const)('blocks the %s payload over 1000 UTF-8 bytes before dispatch', async (_label, text) => {
    const request = vi.fn();
    const beforeDispatch = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(true);
    await expect(new InstagramMessagingClient(request).sendMessage('account-1', 'peer-1', text, 'token', beforeDispatch))
      .resolves.toEqual({ outcome: 'BLOCKED', reason: 'CHANNEL_CONTEXT_CHANGED' });
    expect(beforeDispatch).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(request).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('preserves a successful acknowledgement without a usable message id as ACK_WITHOUT_ID', async () => {
    const request = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(response({ recipient_id: 'peer-1' }));
    await expect(new InstagramMessagingClient(request).sendMessage('account-1', 'peer-1', 'hello', 'token'))
      .resolves.toEqual({ outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'ACK_WITHOUT_ID', legacyCode: 'INSTAGRAM_ACK_WITHOUT_ID' });
    expect(request).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('accepts the returned message id when any returned recipient matches the captured peer', async () => {
    const request = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(response({ recipient_id: 'peer-1', message_id: 'mid-1' }));
    await expect(new InstagramMessagingClient(request).sendMessage('account-1', 'peer-1', 'hello', 'token'))
      .resolves.toEqual({ outcome: 'ACCEPTED', externalMessageId: 'mid-1' });
  });

  it.each([{ recipient_id: 'other-peer', message_id: 'mid-1' }, { recipient_id: 42, message_id: 'mid-1' }])(
    'keeps contradictory or malformed recipient acknowledgement unknown', async (body) => {
      const request = vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(response(body));
      await expect(new InstagramMessagingClient(request).sendMessage('account-1', 'peer-1', 'hello', 'token'))
        .resolves.toEqual({ outcome: 'UNKNOWN', evidence: 'TRANSPORT_UNCONFIRMED', legacyCode: 'INSTAGRAM_ACK_RECIPIENT_MISMATCH' });
    },
  );
});

import type { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import request from 'supertest';
import { createHmac, randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { performance } from 'node:perf_hooks';
import { cpus, totalmem } from 'node:os';
import { Prisma, PrismaClient } from '@prisma/client';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { claimWhatsAppBusinessResource } from '../src/domain/channels/business-resource-ownership';
import { ChannelCredentialResolver } from '../src/domain/channels/channel-credential-resolver';
import { PrismaService } from '../src/infra/prisma/prisma.service';
import {
  criarMantenedora, criarUsuario, instagramFake, metaFake, evolutionFake,
  INSTAGRAM_WEBHOOK_SECRET, limparBanco, login, subirApp,
} from './helpers';

type Account = {
  buId: string; actorId: string; email: string; cookie: string; index: number;
  accountId: string; channelId: string; businessIdentityId: string; resourceIdentityId: string;
  scopeReference: string; username: string;
  shortToken: string; accessToken: string; authorizationReference: string;
};
type CohortRow = { buId: string; conversationId: string; peerId: string; routeId: string; account: Account; createdAt: Date;
  originalMessageId: string; originalExternalId: string };
type QueryEvent = { query: string; params: string; duration: number };
type PlanNode = { 'Node Type'?: string; 'Relation Name'?: string; Alias?: string; 'Index Name'?: string;
  'Actual Rows'?: number; 'Actual Loops'?: number; 'Rows Removed by Filter'?: number;
  'Rows Removed by Index Recheck'?: number; 'Index Cond'?: string; 'Recheck Cond'?: string;
  Filter?: string; Plans?: PlanNode[] };
type ClaimResult = { ok: true; value: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof claimWhatsAppBusinessResource>> }
  | { ok: false; error: unknown };
type ConsumerResponseBody = {
  conversa: { id: string; display?: { platform?: string; instagramUsername?: string | null } | null };
  messages: Array<{ direction: string; channelOrigin: string }>;
  paginacao: { limite: number };
  schemaVersion: number;
  options: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
  total: number; pagina: number; temMais: boolean;
  conversations: Array<{ id: string; display?: { platform?: string; instagramUsername?: string | null } | null }>;
};

// Bound each case and the shared seed/teardown: 20k native row inserts plus
// 400 isolated and 400 measured BFF calls are intentionally retained work.
const SETUP_TIMEOUT_MS = 15 * 60_000;
const waitFor = async <T>(signal: Promise<T>, label: string, timeoutMs = 15_000): Promise<T> => {
  let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
  try {
    return await Promise.race([signal, new Promise<T>((_, reject) => {
      timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error(`Timed out waiting for ${label} after ${timeoutMs}ms`)), timeoutMs);
    })]);
  } finally { if (timer) clearTimeout(timer); }
};
const percentile95 = (values: number[]) => {
  if (values.length !== 100) throw new Error(`Expected 100 measured samples, received ${values.length}.`);
  return [...values].sort((a, b) => a - b)[Math.ceil(values.length * 0.95) - 1]!;
};
const signatureFor = (body: string) => `sha256=${createHmac('sha256', INSTAGRAM_WEBHOOK_SECRET).update(body).digest('hex')}`;
const domainQuery = (query: string) => /\b(FROM|JOIN|UPDATE|INSERT INTO|DELETE FROM)\s+(?:"public"\.)?(?:"?(?:conversations|conversation_routes|contact_identities|contacts|messages|bu_channels|business_channel_identities|provider_resource_identities|channel_credentials|instagram_authorization_attempts|initial_message_intents|dispatch_admissions)"?)/i.test(query);

/**
 * CP5 scale/concurrency evidence. All reads/writes enter through the real Nest
 * application and PostgreSQL. Only the already-declared Instagram/Meta/Evolution
 * provider HTTP ports are fixed fakes. This suite retains its 20k cohort until
 * every EPS, replay, RLS and batch observation has finished.
 */
describe.sequential('Instagram CP5 scale and concurrency (native Nest/PostgreSQL)', () => {
  let app: INestApplication;
  let prisma: PrismaService;
  const accountsByBu: Account[][] = [[], []];
  let buIds: string[] = [];
  const cookiesByBu: string[][] = [[], []];
  const rowsByBu: CohortRow[][] = [[], []];
  const originalRowsByBu: CohortRow[][] = [[], []];
  let queryEvents: QueryEvent[] = [];
  let cohortReady = false;
  const providerCounters = () => ({ instagram: instagramFake.calls.length,
    meta: metaFake.providerCalls, evolution: evolutionFake.providerCalls });
  const expectProviderCountersUnchanged = (before: ReturnType<typeof providerCounters>) => {
    expect(providerCounters()).toEqual(before);
  };
  const databaseResourceSnapshot = async () => {
    const [databaseRows, activityByState, sizeRows] = await Promise.all([
      prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ numbackends: number; xact_commit: bigint; xact_rollback: bigint;
        blks_read: bigint; blks_hit: bigint; tup_returned: bigint; tup_fetched: bigint;
        tup_inserted: bigint; tup_updated: bigint; tup_deleted: bigint; temp_files: bigint;
        temp_bytes: bigint; deadlocks: bigint; conflicts: bigint }>>`
        SELECT numbackends, xact_commit, xact_rollback, blks_read, blks_hit, tup_returned, tup_fetched,
          tup_inserted, tup_updated, tup_deleted, temp_files, temp_bytes, deadlocks, conflicts
        FROM pg_stat_database WHERE datname = current_database()`,
      prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ state: string | null; wait_event_type: string | null; sessions: number }>>`
        SELECT state, wait_event_type, count(*)::int AS sessions FROM pg_stat_activity
        WHERE datname = current_database() AND pid <> pg_backend_pid()
        GROUP BY state, wait_event_type ORDER BY state NULLS FIRST, wait_event_type NULLS FIRST`,
      prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ database_size_bytes: bigint }>>`SELECT pg_database_size(current_database()) AS database_size_bytes`,
    ]);
    const row = databaseRows[0];
    const size = sizeRows[0];
    if (!row || !size) throw new Error('Owned validation PostgreSQL did not return its database resource snapshot.');
    return { sampledAt: new Date().toISOString(), databaseSizeBytes: Number(size.database_size_bytes),
      activeBackendCount: row.numbackends, activityByStateAndWaitType: activityByState,
      cumulativeCounters: { committedTransactions: Number(row.xact_commit), rolledBackTransactions: Number(row.xact_rollback),
        blocksRead: Number(row.blks_read), blocksHit: Number(row.blks_hit), tuplesReturned: Number(row.tup_returned),
        tuplesFetched: Number(row.tup_fetched), tuplesInserted: Number(row.tup_inserted), tuplesUpdated: Number(row.tup_updated),
        tuplesDeleted: Number(row.tup_deleted), temporaryFiles: Number(row.temp_files), temporaryBytes: Number(row.temp_bytes),
        deadlocks: Number(row.deadlocks), conflicts: Number(row.conflicts) } };
  };
  const assertNoTimelineNPlusOne = (events: QueryEvent[]) => {
    const timelineReads = events.filter(({ query }) => /\bFROM\s+(?:"public"\.)?"?messages"?\b/i.test(query)
      && /message_type/i.test(query) && /author_name/i.test(query)
      && /ORDER BY[\s\S]*created_at[\s\S]*id[\s\S]*LIMIT/i.test(query));
    expect(timelineReads.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);
  };
  const flattenPlan = (root: PlanNode): PlanNode[] => {
    const nodes = [root];
    for (const child of root.Plans ?? []) nodes.push(...flattenPlan(child));
    return nodes;
  };
  const usesDeclaredIndex = (root: PlanNode, relation: string, indexes: Array<{ tablename: string; indexname: string }>,
    alias?: string, condition?: RegExp) => flattenPlan(root)
    .filter((node) => node['Relation Name'] === relation && (alias === undefined || node.Alias === alias))
    .some((scan) => flattenPlan(scan).some((node) => Boolean(node['Index Name'])
      && indexes.some((index) => index.tablename === relation && index.indexname === node['Index Name'])
      && (!condition || condition.test(node['Index Cond'] ?? ''))));
  const scanWork = (node: PlanNode) => (node['Actual Loops'] ?? 1)
    * ((node['Actual Rows'] ?? 0) + (node['Rows Removed by Filter'] ?? 0) + (node['Rows Removed by Index Recheck'] ?? 0));
  const planNodeEvidence = (node: PlanNode) => ({ alias: node.Alias ?? null, table: node['Relation Name'] ?? null,
    nodeType: node['Node Type'] ?? null, index: node['Index Name'] ?? null, indexCondition: node['Index Cond'] ?? null,
    recheckCondition: node['Recheck Cond'] ?? null, filter: node.Filter ?? null, actualRowsPerLoop: node['Actual Rows'] ?? 0,
    loops: node['Actual Loops'] ?? 1, removedByFilterPerLoop: node['Rows Removed by Filter'] ?? 0,
    removedByIndexRecheckPerLoop: node['Rows Removed by Index Recheck'] ?? 0, totalObservedWork: scanWork(node) });
  const logPlanDiagnostic = (source: string, sql: string, params: unknown[], explainAnalyzeJson: unknown, root: PlanNode) => {
    const diagnostic = { source, capturedSql: sql, capturedParams: params, explainAnalyzeJson,
      relevantScanNodes: flattenPlan(root).filter((node) => ['conversation_routes', 'contact_identities',
        'business_channel_identities'].includes(node['Relation Name'] ?? '')).map(planNodeEvidence) };
    const serialized = JSON.stringify(diagnostic);
    if (Buffer.byteLength(serialized, 'utf8') > 256 * 1024) {
      throw new Error(`Bounded ${source} EXPLAIN diagnostic exceeded its 256 KiB evidence limit.`);
    }
    console.info(`[CP5-EXPLAIN-DIAGNOSTIC]${serialized}`);
  };
  const accountBalancedSampleRows = () => rowsByBu.map((rows, buIndex) => {
    const rowsByChannel = new Map<string, CohortRow[]>();
    for (const row of rows) rowsByChannel.set(row.account.channelId,
      [...(rowsByChannel.get(row.account.channelId) ?? []), row]);
    const selected = accountsByBu[buIndex]!.flatMap((account, accountIndex) =>
      rowsByChannel.get(account.channelId)!.slice(0, accountIndex < 2 ? 5 : 4));
    expect(selected).toHaveLength(50);
    return selected;
  });

  const bff = (cookie: string) => ({
    get: (path: string) => request(app.getHttpServer()).get(path).set('cookie', cookie),
    post: (path: string) => request(app.getHttpServer()).post(path).set('cookie', cookie).set('x-requested-with', 'teste'),
  });
  const signedBatch = (accountId: string, messages: Array<{ peer: string; id: string; text: string }>) => {
    const body = JSON.stringify({ object: 'instagram', entry: [{ id: accountId, messaging: messages.map(({ peer, id, text }) => ({
      sender: { id: peer }, recipient: { id: accountId }, timestamp: Date.now(), message: { mid: id, text },
    })) }] });
    return request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/instagram').set('content-type', 'application/json')
      .set('x-hub-signature-256', signatureFor(body)).send(body);
  };
  const authorizeAccount = async (account: Account, actorCookie: string) => {
    const shortToken = `synthetic-cp5-short-${randomUUID()}`;
    const accessToken = `synthetic-cp5-long-${randomUUID()}`;
    const authorizationReference = `synthetic-cp5-authref-${randomUUID()}`;
    instagramFake.shortEnvelope = { data: [{ access_token: shortToken, user_id: authorizationReference,
      permissions: 'instagram_business_basic,instagram_business_manage_messages' }] };
    instagramFake.longEnvelope = { access_token: accessToken, expires_in: 7200 };
    const username = `cp5_${account.index}_${account.buId.slice(-5)}`;
    instagramFake.meEnvelope = { user_id: account.accountId, username };
    instagramFake.subscribed = false;
    const reservation = await bff(actorCookie).post(`/api/bff/business-units/${account.buId}/instagram-authorizations`)
      .set('idempotency-key', randomUUID()).send({ operation: 'CONNECT' }).expect(201);
    const state = new URL(reservation.body.authorizationUrl).searchParams.get('state');
    if (!state) throw new Error('Real OAuth BFF reservation did not return its state parameter.');
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/callback').set('cookie', actorCookie)
      .query({ state, code: `synthetic-cp5-oauth-${randomUUID()}` }).expect(303);
    await bff(actorCookie).post(`/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${reservation.body.id}/confirm`)
      .set('idempotency-key', randomUUID()).send({}).expect(200);
    const attempt = await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: reservation.body.id } });
    if (!attempt.channelId) throw new Error('OAuth confirmation did not map a durable Instagram channel.');
    const channel = await prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: attempt.channelId } });
    if (!channel.businessIdentityId) throw new Error('OAuth confirmation did not map a business identity.');
    const resource = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { currentChannelId: channel.id, provider: 'INSTAGRAM' } });
    const business = await prisma.businessChannelIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: channel.businessIdentityId } });
    return { ...account, shortToken, accessToken, authorizationReference, username,
      channelId: channel.id, businessIdentityId: business.id, resourceIdentityId: resource.id,
      scopeReference: business.scopeReference };
  };
  const acceptOriginalInbound = async (account: Account, peer: string, externalId: string) => {
    const response = await signedBatch(account.accountId, [{ peer, id: externalId, text: 'Mensagem original recebida para prova de rota.' }]).expect(200);
    const route = await prisma.conversationRoute.findFirstOrThrow({ where: {
      buId: account.buId, platform: 'INSTAGRAM', businessIdentityId: account.businessIdentityId,
      contactIdentity: { externalPeerId: peer, namespace: 'INSTAGRAM_LOGIN', scopeReference: account.scopeReference },
    }, include: { conversation: true, contactIdentity: true, businessIdentity: true } });
    const original = await prisma.message.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { buId: account.buId,
      conversationId: route.conversationId, externalId, direction: 'IN', channelOrigin: 'INSTAGRAM',
      providerResourceIdentityId: account.resourceIdentityId, status: 'RECEBIDA', messageType: 'TEXTO' } });
    expect(response.status).toBe(200);
    expect(original.content).toBe('Mensagem original recebida para prova de rota.');
    expect(original.officialInboundAt).toBeInstanceOf(Date);
    expect(Date.now() - original.officialInboundAt!.getTime()).toBeLessThan(24 * 60 * 60 * 1_000);
    return { route, original };
  };
  const measureRequest = async (run: () => Promise<{ status: number; body: unknown }>) => {
    const start = queryEvents.length;
    const started = performance.now();
    const rawResponse = await run();
    const response = { ...rawResponse, body: rawResponse.body as ConsumerResponseBody };
    const elapsedMs = performance.now() - started;
    const ownEvents = queryEvents.slice(start);
    const domainQueries = ownEvents.filter(({ query }) => domainQuery(query)).length;
    assertNoTimelineNPlusOne(ownEvents);
    expect(response.status).toBe(200);
    return { response, elapsedMs, domainQueries, ownEvents };
  };
  const orderedPageIds = async (buId: string, page: 1 | 2) => prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ id: string }>>`
    SELECT id FROM conversations
    WHERE bu_id = ${buId} AND contact_type = 'PESSOA'::"ContactType"
    ORDER BY COALESCE(last_message_at, created_at) DESC, id DESC
    OFFSET ${(page - 1) * 50} LIMIT 50`;
  const assertRetainedCohort = async () => {
    const counts = await Promise.all(buIds.map(async (buId, index) => {
      const originalMessages = JSON.stringify(rowsByBu[index]!.map(({ originalMessageId, originalExternalId, conversationId, account }) => ({
        id: originalMessageId, externalId: originalExternalId, conversationId, resourceIdentityId: account.resourceIdentityId,
      })));
      const [count] = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ conversations: bigint; routes: bigint; original_in: bigint }>>`
        WITH original_ids AS (
          SELECT item->>'id' AS id, item->>'externalId' AS external_id,
            item->>'conversationId' AS conversation_id, item->>'resourceIdentityId' AS resource_identity_id
          FROM jsonb_array_elements(${originalMessages}::jsonb) AS original_entry(item)
        ),
        cohort AS (
          SELECT count(DISTINCT cv.id) AS conversations, count(DISTINCT route.id) AS routes
          FROM conversations cv JOIN conversation_routes route ON route.conversation_id = cv.id AND route.bu_id = cv.bu_id
          JOIN contact_identities peer ON peer.id = route.contact_identity_id AND peer.bu_id = route.bu_id
          WHERE cv.bu_id = ${buId} AND route.platform = 'INSTAGRAM'
            AND route.contact_scope_reference = peer.scope_reference
            AND route.business_scope_reference = peer.scope_reference
            AND peer.external_peer_id LIKE 'synthetic-cp5-%peer-%'
        ), original_in AS (
          SELECT count(DISTINCT message.id) AS original_in
          FROM original_ids ids JOIN messages message ON message.id::text = ids.id AND message.external_id = ids.external_id
            AND message.conversation_id::text = ids.conversation_id
            AND message.provider_resource_identity_id::text = ids.resource_identity_id
          JOIN conversations cv ON cv.id = message.conversation_id AND cv.bu_id = message.bu_id
          JOIN conversation_routes route ON route.conversation_id = cv.id AND route.bu_id = cv.bu_id
          JOIN contact_identities peer ON peer.id = route.contact_identity_id AND peer.bu_id = route.bu_id
          JOIN provider_resource_identities resource ON resource.id = message.provider_resource_identity_id
            AND resource.bu_id = cv.bu_id AND resource.provider = 'INSTAGRAM' AND resource.current_channel_id IS NOT NULL
            AND resource.business_identity_id = route.business_identity_id
          JOIN bu_channels current_channel ON current_channel.id = resource.current_channel_id AND current_channel.bu_id = cv.bu_id
            AND current_channel.business_identity_id = route.business_identity_id AND current_channel.type = 'INSTAGRAM'
          WHERE cv.bu_id = ${buId} AND route.platform = 'INSTAGRAM'
            AND route.contact_scope_reference = peer.scope_reference
            AND route.business_scope_reference = peer.scope_reference
            AND peer.external_peer_id LIKE 'synthetic-cp5-%peer-%'
            AND message.direction = 'IN' AND message.channel_origin = 'INSTAGRAM'
        )
        SELECT cohort.conversations, cohort.routes, original_in.original_in FROM cohort CROSS JOIN original_in`;
      return [Number(count?.conversations ?? 0n), Number(count?.routes ?? 0n), Number(count?.original_in ?? 0n)];
    }));
    expect(counts).toEqual([[10_000, 10_000, 10_000], [10_000, 10_000, 10_000]]);
  };

  const seedScaleCohort = async () => {
    const actors = await Promise.all(buIds.map((buId, buIndex) => prisma.businessUnit.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: buId },
      select: { mantenedoraId: true, setorId: true, phone: true } }).then((unit) => ({ buIndex, unit }))));

    // One proved WhatsApp resource per BU with the two permitted adapters.
    for (const { buIndex, unit } of actors) {
      const phone = `551199${String(70000000 + buIndex).padStart(8, '0')}`;
      await prisma.businessUnit.update({ where: { id: buIds[buIndex] }, data: { phone: null } });
      const officialId = `synthetic-cp5-wa-official-${buIndex}-${randomUUID()}`;
      const evolutionId = `synthetic-cp5-wa-evolution-${buIndex}-${randomUUID()}`;
      const official = await prisma.buChannel.create({ data: { buId: buIds[buIndex]!, type: 'OFICIAL', status: 'CONECTADO',
        phoneNumberId: officialId, wabaId: `synthetic-cp5-waba-${buIndex}-${randomUUID()}`, ownNumberVerified: true,
        verifiedEpoch: 0, providerConnectionState: 'SUBSCRIBED', receiptVerifiedAt: new Date() } });
      const officialResource = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.create({ data: { provider: 'OFICIAL', externalResourceId: officialId,
        buId: buIds[buIndex]!, currentChannelId: official.id, managedLifecycle: true, cleanupState: 'ACTIVE' } });
      const evolution = await prisma.buChannel.create({ data: { buId: buIds[buIndex]!, type: 'NAO_OFICIAL', status: 'CONECTADO',
        evolutionInstanceName: evolutionId, requiresWindow: false, ownNumberVerified: true, verifiedEpoch: 0,
        providerConnectionState: 'CONNECTED', receiptVerifiedAt: new Date() } });
      await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.create({ data: { provider: 'NAO_OFICIAL', externalResourceId: evolutionId,
        buId: buIds[buIndex]!, currentChannelId: evolution.id, managedLifecycle: true, cleanupState: 'ACTIVE' } });
      const waba = `synthetic-cp5-observed-waba-${buIndex}`;
      metaFake.wabaRecursos = [...metaFake.wabaRecursos, { wabaId: waba, phoneNumberId: officialId, telefone: phone }];
      metaFake.credentialByPhone.set(officialId, 'fixture-scoped-token');
      const observed = await metaFake.confirmarNumero(waba, officialId, 'fixture-scoped-token');
      expect(observed).toMatchObject({ id: officialId, telefone: phone });
      const officialIdentity = await prisma.$transaction((tx) => claimWhatsAppBusinessResource(tx, {
        buId: buIds[buIndex]!, channelId: official.id, providerResourceIdentityId: officialResource.id,
        adapter: 'OFICIAL', provedPhone: observed!.telefone,
      }));
      // Evolution's owner is observed from its fixed provider boundary and must agree with the same BU phone.
      evolutionFake.ownerPhone = phone;
      evolutionFake.inventory.set(evolutionId, { name: evolutionId, ownerJid: `${phone}@s.whatsapp.net`, connectionStatus: 'open' });
      const evolutionObservation = await evolutionFake.consultarInstancia(evolutionId);
      expect(evolutionObservation?.ownerJid).toBe(`${phone}@s.whatsapp.net`);
      const evolutionResource = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: {
        provider_externalResourceId: { provider: 'NAO_OFICIAL', externalResourceId: evolutionId },
      } });
      const evolutionIdentity = await prisma.$transaction((tx) => claimWhatsAppBusinessResource(tx, {
        buId: buIds[buIndex]!, channelId: evolution.id, providerResourceIdentityId: evolutionResource.id,
        adapter: 'NAO_OFICIAL', provedPhone: phone,
      }));
      expect(evolutionIdentity.id).toBe(officialIdentity.id);
      expect(await prisma.buChannel.count({ where: { buId: buIds[buIndex]!, businessIdentityId: officialIdentity.id } })).toBe(2);
      expect(unit.mantenedoraId).toBeTruthy();
    }

    const prepared: Array<{ row: CohortRow; contactId: string; identityId: string; routeId: string; createdAt: Date }> = [];
    const baseTime = new Date(Date.now() - 60 * 60_000);
    for (let buIndex = 0; buIndex < 2; buIndex += 1) {
      const buAccounts = accountsByBu[buIndex]!;
      const records: typeof prepared = [];
      for (let index = 0; index < 9_988; index += 1) {
        const account = buAccounts[index < 1_000 ? 0 : index % buAccounts.length]!;
        const conversationId = randomUUID();
        const peerId = `synthetic-cp5-peer-${buIndex}-${index}-${randomUUID()}`;
        const createdAt = new Date(baseTime.getTime() + buIndex * 20_000 + index);
        const routeId = randomUUID();
        const originalMessageId = randomUUID();
        const originalExternalId = `synthetic-cp5-original-in-${buIndex}-${index}-${randomUUID()}`;
        records.push({ row: { buId: buIds[buIndex]!, conversationId, peerId, routeId, account, createdAt,
          originalMessageId, originalExternalId }, contactId: randomUUID(), identityId: randomUUID(), routeId, createdAt });
      }
      for (let offset = 0; offset < records.length; offset += 750) {
        const chunk = records.slice(offset, offset + 750);
        const accountFacts = chunk.map((record) => record.row.account);
        await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
          await tx.contact.createMany({ data: chunk.map(({ row, contactId }) => ({ id: contactId, buId: row.buId, displayName: `Synthetic IG peer ${row.peerId}` })) });
          await tx.contactIdentity.createMany({ data: chunk.map(({ row, contactId, identityId }) => ({
            id: identityId, buId: row.buId, contactId, platform: 'INSTAGRAM', namespace: 'INSTAGRAM_LOGIN',
            scopeReference: row.account.scopeReference, externalPeerId: row.peerId,
          })) });
          await tx.conversation.createMany({ data: chunk.map(({ row, contactId, createdAt }) => ({
            id: row.conversationId, buId: row.buId, contactId, contact: row.peerId, contactType: 'PESSOA',
            displayName: `Synthetic IG peer ${row.peerId}`, status: 'QUEUED', unreadCount: 1,
            lastMessageAt: createdAt, createdAt,
          })) });
          await tx.conversationRoute.createMany({ data: chunk.map(({ row, contactId, identityId, routeId }) => ({
            id: routeId, buId: row.buId, conversationId: row.conversationId, contactId,
            contactIdentityId: identityId, platform: 'INSTAGRAM', contactScopeReference: row.account.scopeReference,
            businessScopeReference: row.account.scopeReference, businessIdentityId: row.account.businessIdentityId,
          })) });
          await tx.message.createMany({ data: chunk.map(({ row, createdAt }) => ({
            id: row.originalMessageId, buId: row.buId, conversationId: row.conversationId, direction: 'IN', messageType: 'TEXTO',
            content: 'Synthetic original Instagram inbound row for the qualified current-resource route.',
            channelOrigin: 'INSTAGRAM', status: 'RECEBIDA', providerResourceIdentityId: row.account.resourceIdentityId,
            externalId: row.originalExternalId, createdAt, officialInboundAt: createdAt,
          })) });
        });
        // Keep the distinct account association visible to the test and make a malformed mapping fail early.
        expect(accountFacts.every((account) => account.buId === buIds[buIndex])).toBe(true);
      }
      rowsByBu[buIndex] = [...records.map(({ row }) => row), ...originalRowsByBu[buIndex]!];
      expect(rowsByBu[buIndex]).toHaveLength(10_000);
      prepared.push(...records);
    }
    cohortReady = true;
    await assertRetainedCohort();
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId: { in: buIds }, direction: 'IN', channelOrigin: 'INSTAGRAM' } })).toBe(20_000);

  };

  beforeAll(async () => {
    ({ app, prisma } = await subirApp(undefined, true));
    await app.listen(0, '127.0.0.1');
    await limparBanco(prisma);
    queryEvents = [];
    prisma.$on('query', (event) => { queryEvents.push({ query: event.query, params: event.params, duration: event.duration }); });
    instagramFake.afterRequestHook = null;
    metaFake.wabaRecursos = [...metaFake.wabaRecursos];
    const tenants = await Promise.all([
      criarMantenedora(app, 'Instagram CP5 cohort A', `instagram-cp5-a-${randomUUID()}@example.test`),
      criarMantenedora(app, 'Instagram CP5 cohort B', `instagram-cp5-b-${randomUUID()}@example.test`),
    ]);
    buIds = tenants.map(({ businessUnit }) => businessUnit.id);
    for (const [buIndex, tenant] of tenants.entries()) {
      const actors: Array<{ id: string; email: string; cookie: string }> = [
        { id: tenant.owner.id, email: tenant.owner.email, cookie: await login(app, tenant.owner.email) },
      ];
      for (let actorIndex = 1; actorIndex < 3; actorIndex += 1) {
        const email = `instagram-cp5-${buIndex}-${actorIndex}-${randomUUID()}@example.test`;
        const id = await criarUsuario(prisma, tenant.mantenedora.id, tenant.businessUnit.id, email, 'ADMIN');
        actors.push({ id, email, cookie: await login(app, email) });
      }
      cookiesByBu[buIndex] = actors.map(({ cookie }) => cookie);
      const assignments = actors.flatMap((actor, actorIndex) => Array.from({ length: 4 }, (_, localIndex) => ({
        buId: tenant.businessUnit.id, actorId: actor.id, email: actor.email, cookie: actor.cookie,
        index: actorIndex * 4 + localIndex, accountId: `synthetic-cp5-account-${buIndex}-${actorIndex}-${localIndex}-${randomUUID()}`,
        channelId: '', businessIdentityId: '', resourceIdentityId: '', scopeReference: '',
        username: '', shortToken: '', accessToken: '', authorizationReference: '',
      })));
      const accounts: Account[] = [];
      for (const initial of assignments) accounts.push(await authorizeAccount(initial, initial.cookie));
      accountsByBu[buIndex] = accounts;
      originalRowsByBu[buIndex] = [];
      for (const account of accounts) {
        const peer = `synthetic-cp5-original-peer-${account.index}-${randomUUID()}`;
        const externalId = `synthetic-cp5-original-message-${account.index}-${randomUUID()}`;
        const { route, original } = await acceptOriginalInbound(account, peer, externalId);
        expect(original.externalId).toBe(externalId);
        originalRowsByBu[buIndex]!.push({ buId: account.buId, conversationId: route.conversationId, peerId: peer,
          routeId: route.id, account, createdAt: route.conversation.lastMessageAt ?? route.conversation.createdAt,
          originalMessageId: original.id, originalExternalId: externalId });
        const credential = await new ChannelCredentialResolver(prisma).resolve(account.channelId, 'verify');
        expect(credential).toMatchObject({ channelId: account.channelId, buId: account.buId,
          instagramAccountId: account.accountId, activeCredentialVersion: expect.any(Number) });
        expect(credential.accessToken).toBe(account.accessToken);
        expect(await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.count({ where: { buId: account.buId,
          authorizationReference: account.authorizationReference, professionalUserId: account.accountId } })).toBe(1);
      }
    }
    expect(accountsByBu.map((accounts) => accounts.length)).toEqual([12, 12]);
    expect(cookiesByBu.map((cookies) => cookies.length)).toEqual([3, 3]);
    expect(new Set(accountsByBu.flat().map(({ accessToken }) => accessToken)).size).toBe(24);
    expect(new Set(accountsByBu.flat().map(({ shortToken }) => shortToken)).size).toBe(24);
    expect(new Set(accountsByBu.flat().map(({ authorizationReference }) => authorizationReference)).size).toBe(24);
    await seedScaleCohort();
    expect(queryEvents.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  }, SETUP_TIMEOUT_MS);

  afterAll(async () => {
    if (prisma) await limparBanco(prisma);
    await app?.close();
  }, SETUP_TIMEOUT_MS);

  it('accepts exactly 1000 supplementary code points and preflights a signed mixed batch at 1001', async () => {
    await assertRetainedCohort();
    const account = accountsByBu[0]![0]!;
    const acceptedTarget = rowsByBu[0]!.find(({ account: rowAccount }) => rowAccount.accountId === account.accountId);
    if (!acceptedTarget) throw new Error('CP5 Unicode positive case did not find its retained current-account cohort row.');
    const acceptedId = `synthetic-cp5-unicode-1000-${randomUUID()}`;
    const acceptedText = '🙂'.repeat(1_000);
    const unreadBeforeAcceptedEvent = await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({
      where: { id: acceptedTarget.conversationId }, select: { unreadCount: true },
    });
    const originalBeforeAcceptedEvent = await prisma.message.findUniqueOrThrow({
      where: { id: acceptedTarget.originalMessageId }, select: { id: true, externalId: true,
        conversationId: true, providerResourceIdentityId: true, direction: true, channelOrigin: true },
    });
    const providerCallsBefore = providerCounters();
    const acceptedResponse = await signedBatch(account.accountId,
      [{ peer: acceptedTarget.peerId, id: acceptedId, text: acceptedText }]);
    expect(acceptedResponse.status).toBe(200);
    const acceptedMessage = await prisma.message.findUniqueOrThrow({
      where: { providerResourceIdentityId_externalId: { providerResourceIdentityId: account.resourceIdentityId, externalId: acceptedId } },
      include: { conversation: true },
    });
    expect(acceptedMessage.content).toBe(acceptedText);
    expect(Array.from(acceptedMessage.content ?? '')).toHaveLength(1_000);
    expect(acceptedMessage).toMatchObject({ buId: account.buId, direction: 'IN', channelOrigin: 'INSTAGRAM',
      messageType: 'TEXTO', status: 'RECEBIDA', providerResourceIdentityId: account.resourceIdentityId });
    const acceptedRoute = await prisma.conversationRoute.findFirstOrThrow({ where: {
      id: acceptedTarget.routeId, buId: account.buId, conversationId: acceptedMessage.conversationId, platform: 'INSTAGRAM',
      contactIdentity: { externalPeerId: acceptedTarget.peerId, namespace: 'INSTAGRAM_LOGIN', scopeReference: account.scopeReference },
    }, include: { contactIdentity: true, businessIdentity: true } });
    expect(acceptedRoute).toMatchObject({ buId: account.buId, conversationId: acceptedMessage.conversationId,
      platform: 'INSTAGRAM', contactIdentity: { externalPeerId: acceptedTarget.peerId, buId: account.buId,
        namespace: 'INSTAGRAM_LOGIN', scopeReference: account.scopeReference },
      businessIdentity: { id: account.businessIdentityId, buId: account.buId } });
    expect(acceptedMessage.conversationId).toBe(acceptedTarget.conversationId);
    expect(acceptedMessage.conversation.unreadCount).toBe(unreadBeforeAcceptedEvent.unreadCount + 1);
    expect(await prisma.message.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: acceptedTarget.originalMessageId }, select: {
      id: true, externalId: true, conversationId: true, providerResourceIdentityId: true, direction: true, channelOrigin: true,
    } })).toEqual(originalBeforeAcceptedEvent);
    expect(await prisma.conversationRoute.count({ where: { buId: account.buId, conversationId: acceptedTarget.conversationId,
      contactIdentity: { externalPeerId: acceptedTarget.peerId } } })).toBe(1);
    expectProviderCountersUnchanged(providerCallsBefore);

    const rejectedPeer = `synthetic-cp5-unicode-1001-peer-${randomUUID()}`;
    const rejectedValidPeer = `synthetic-cp5-unicode-batch-valid-peer-${randomUUID()}`;
    const rejectedValidId = `synthetic-cp5-unicode-batch-valid-${randomUUID()}`;
    const rejectedId = `synthetic-cp5-unicode-1001-${randomUUID()}`;
    const oversizedText = '🙂'.repeat(1_001);
    const snapshot = async () => {
      const [messages, conversations, routes, contacts, identities, webhookEvents, unread, channel, resource] = await Promise.all([
        prisma.message.count({ where: { buId: account.buId, direction: 'IN', channelOrigin: 'INSTAGRAM' } }),
        prisma.conversation.count({ where: { buId: account.buId } }),
        prisma.conversationRoute.count({ where: { buId: account.buId, platform: 'INSTAGRAM' } }),
        prisma.contact.count({ where: { buId: account.buId } }),
        prisma.contactIdentity.count({ where: { buId: account.buId, platform: 'INSTAGRAM' } }),
        prisma.webhookEvent.count({ where: { buId: account.buId, channel: 'INSTAGRAM' } }),
        prisma.conversation.aggregate({ where: { buId: account.buId }, _sum: { unreadCount: true } }),
        prisma.buChannel.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: account.channelId }, select: { status: true,
          generation: true, pairingEpoch: true, verifiedEpoch: true, ownNumberVerified: true,
          providerConnectionState: true, receiptVerifiedAt: true, businessIdentityId: true } }),
        prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: account.resourceIdentityId }, select: {
          id: true, buId: true, provider: true, externalResourceId: true, currentChannelId: true,
          businessIdentityId: true, createdAt: true, retiredAt: true, managedLifecycle: true, unverifiedStaging: true,
          cleanupState: true, cleanupLeaseToken: true, cleanupLeaseUntil: true,
          instagramOperationLeaseToken: true, instagramOperationLeaseUntil: true, instagramLastSubscriptionAttemptAt: true,
          instagramSubscriptionPostDispatchStartedAt: true } }),
      ]);
      return { messages, conversations, routes, contacts, identities, webhookEvents,
        unread: unread._sum.unreadCount, channel, resource, providerCounters: providerCounters() };
    };
    const beforeRejectedBatch = await snapshot();
    const rejectedResponse = await signedBatch(account.accountId, [
      { peer: rejectedValidPeer, id: rejectedValidId, text: 'A valid event placed before the oversized event.' },
      { peer: rejectedPeer, id: rejectedId, text: oversizedText },
    ]);
    expect(rejectedResponse.status).toBe(413);
    expect(rejectedResponse.body).toMatchObject({ code: 'WEBHOOK_TEXT_TOO_LARGE' });
    expect(await snapshot()).toEqual(beforeRejectedBatch);
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { externalId: { in: [rejectedValidId, rejectedId] } } })).toBe(0);
    expect(await prisma.contactIdentity.count({ where: { buId: account.buId,
      externalPeerId: { in: [rejectedValidPeer, rejectedPeer] } } })).toBe(0);
    expect(await prisma.conversationRoute.count({ where: { buId: account.buId,
      contactIdentity: { externalPeerId: { in: [rejectedValidPeer, rejectedPeer] } } } })).toBe(0);
    expectProviderCountersUnchanged(providerCallsBefore);
    expect(await prisma.conversationRoute.count({ where: { buId: { in: buIds }, platform: 'INSTAGRAM' } })).toBe(20_000);
  }, SETUP_TIMEOUT_MS);

  it('measures real EPS BFF reads over the retained 20k Instagram cohort', async () => {
    await assertRetainedCohort();
    const providerCallsBeforeEps = providerCounters();
    const resourceWindowStartedAt = new Date().toISOString();
    const resourceWindowStartedMonotonicMs = performance.now();
    const processCpuBeforeEps = process.cpuUsage();
    const processMemoryBeforeEps = process.memoryUsage();
    const databaseResourcesBeforeEps = await databaseResourceSnapshot();
    // EXPLAIN the actual captured BFF page SQL with its original bound parameters.
    queryEvents.length = 0;
    const listSample = await bff(cookiesByBu[0]![0]!).get(`/api/bff/business-units/${buIds[0]}/conversations`)
      .query({ pagina: 1, limite: 50 }).expect(200);
    expect(listSample.body.conversations).toHaveLength(50);
    const routeSql = queryEvents.find(({ query }) => /\b(?:FROM|JOIN)\s+(?:"public"\.)?"?conversation_routes"?/i.test(query)
      && /SELECT/i.test(query) && !/^EXPLAIN/i.test(query));
    if (!routeSql) throw new Error('Actual BFF list page did not emit its batched conversation-route SQL.');
    const params = JSON.parse(routeSql.params) as unknown[];
    const planRows = await prisma.$queryRawUnsafe<Array<{ 'QUERY PLAN': Array<{ Plan: PlanNode }> }>>(
      `EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS, FORMAT JSON) ${routeSql.query}`, ...params);
    const rootPlan = planRows[0]?.['QUERY PLAN']?.[0]?.Plan;
    if (!rootPlan) throw new Error('PostgreSQL returned no JSON plan for the captured real BFF route query.');
    logPlanDiagnostic('BFF-list-page-route-display', routeSql.query, params, planRows[0]?.['QUERY PLAN'], rootPlan);
    const planNodes = flattenPlan(rootPlan);
    const routeScans = planNodes.filter((node) => node['Relation Name'] === 'conversation_routes');
    const mainRouteScans = routeScans.filter((node) => node.Alias === 'r');
    const lateralRouteScans = routeScans.filter((node) => node.Alias === 'rr');
    const listPeerScans = planNodes.filter((node) => node['Relation Name'] === 'contact_identities' && node.Alias === 'ci');
    expect(routeSql.query).toMatch(/contact_identities/i);
    expect(routeSql.query).toMatch(/business_channel_identities/i);
    expect(routeSql.query).toMatch(/r\.platform\s*=\s*'INSTAGRAM'/i);
    expect(routeSql.query).toMatch(/ci\.namespace\s*=\s*'INSTAGRAM_LOGIN'/i);
    expect(routeSql.query).toMatch(/b\.namespace\s*=\s*'INSTAGRAM_LOGIN'/i);
    expect(routeSql.query).toMatch(/cv\.id\s+IN/i);
    expect(routeSql.query).toMatch(/ci\.bu_id\s*=\s*r\.bu_id/i);
    expect(routeSql.query).toMatch(/ci\.id\s*=\s*r\.contact_identity_id/i);
    expect(routeSql.query).toMatch(/ci\.scope_reference\s*=\s*r\.contact_scope_reference/i);
    expect(routeSql.query).toMatch(/ci\.platform\s*=\s*r\.platform/i);
    expect(routeSql.query).toMatch(/b\.bu_id\s*=\s*r\.bu_id/i);
    expect(routeSql.query).toMatch(/b\.scope_reference\s*=\s*r\.business_scope_reference/i);
    const routeIndexes = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ tablename: string; indexname: string; indexdef: string }>>`
      SELECT tablename, indexname, indexdef FROM pg_indexes WHERE schemaname = 'public'
        AND tablename IN ('conversation_routes', 'contact_identities', 'business_channel_identities')`;
    for (const table of ['conversation_routes', 'contact_identities', 'business_channel_identities']) {
      expect(routeIndexes.some((index) => index.tablename === table)).toBe(true);
    }
    expect(routeScans.some((node) => Boolean(node['Index Name']) && routeIndexes.some((index) =>
      index.tablename === 'conversation_routes' && index.indexname === node['Index Name']))).toBe(true);
    expect(mainRouteScans.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(lateralRouteScans.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(listPeerScans.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    const routeIndexDefinitions = routeIndexes.filter((index) => index.tablename === 'conversation_routes'
      && /\(conversation_id(?:,|\))/i.test(index.indexdef));
    expect(usesDeclaredIndex(rootPlan, 'conversation_routes', routeIndexDefinitions, 'r', /conversation_id/i)).toBe(true);
    expect(usesDeclaredIndex(rootPlan, 'conversation_routes', routeIndexDefinitions, 'rr', /conversation_id/i)).toBe(true);
    const listPeerPrimaryIdIndex = usesDeclaredIndex(rootPlan, 'contact_identities', routeIndexes, 'ci', /(?:^|\W)id(?:\W|$)/i);
    const listPeerScopedContactBuIndex = listPeerScans.some((node) => {
      const indexName = node['Index Name'];
      const declaredIndex = routeIndexes.some((index) => index.tablename === 'contact_identities'
        && index.indexname === indexName && /\(contact_id, bu_id\)/i.test(index.indexdef));
      const indexCondition = node['Index Cond'] ?? '';
      const residualFilter = node.Filter ?? '';
      return node['Node Type'] === 'Index Scan' && declaredIndex
        && /contact_id\s*=\s*r\.contact_id/i.test(indexCondition)
        && /bu_id\s*=\s*r\.bu_id/i.test(indexCondition)
        && /id\s*=\s*r\.contact_identity_id/i.test(residualFilter)
        && /platform\s*=\s*r\.platform/i.test(residualFilter)
        && /scope_reference\s*=\s*r\.contact_scope_reference/i.test(residualFilter);
    });
    expect(listPeerPrimaryIdIndex || listPeerScopedContactBuIndex).toBe(true);
    expect(mainRouteScans.reduce((total, node) => total + scanWork(node), 0)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(50);
    expect(lateralRouteScans.reduce((total, node) => total + scanWork(node), 0)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(50);
    expect(listPeerScans.reduce((total, node) => total + scanWork(node), 0)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(50);
    expect(routeIndexes.some(({ tablename, indexdef }) => tablename === 'contact_identities'
      && /\(bu_id, platform, namespace, scope_reference, external_peer_id\)/i.test(indexdef))).toBe(true);
    expect(routeIndexes.some(({ tablename, indexdef }) => tablename === 'business_channel_identities'
      && /\(id, bu_id, platform, scope_reference\)/i.test(indexdef))).toBe(true);
    expect(routeIndexes.some(({ tablename, indexdef }) => tablename === 'business_channel_identities'
      && /\(platform, namespace, external_resource_id\)/i.test(indexdef))).toBe(true);
    expect(routeIndexes.some(({ tablename, indexdef }) => tablename === 'conversation_routes'
      && /\(conversation_id, contact_identity_id, business_identity_id\)/i.test(indexdef))).toBe(true);
    const identityOwnerRoutePlans = planNodes.filter((node) => ['conversation_routes', 'contact_identities',
      'business_channel_identities'].includes(node['Relation Name'] ?? '')).map((node) => ({
        ...planNodeEvidence(node),
      }));

    // A second real BFF thread consumer captures the scoped peer/owner display lookup.
    queryEvents.length = 0;
    const threadProbe = await bff(cookiesByBu[0]![0]!).get(`/api/bff/conversations/${rowsByBu[0]![0]!.conversationId}/messages`)
      .query({ limite: 50, marcarLida: false }).expect(200);
    expect(threadProbe.body.conversa.display).toMatchObject({ platform: 'INSTAGRAM', instagramUsername: rowsByBu[0]![0]!.account.username });
    const threadDisplaySql = queryEvents.find(({ query }) => /\b(?:FROM|JOIN)\s+(?:"public"\.)?"?conversation_routes"?/i.test(query)
      && /contact_identities/i.test(query) && /business_channel_identities/i.test(query));
    if (!threadDisplaySql) throw new Error('Actual BFF thread consumer did not emit its scoped peer and business-owner display SQL.');
    const threadDisplayParams = JSON.parse(threadDisplaySql.params) as unknown[];
    const threadPlanRows = await prisma.$queryRawUnsafe<Array<{ 'QUERY PLAN': Array<{ Plan: PlanNode }> }>>(
      `EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS, FORMAT JSON) ${threadDisplaySql.query}`, ...threadDisplayParams);
    const threadPlan = threadPlanRows[0]?.['QUERY PLAN']?.[0]?.Plan;
    if (!threadPlan) throw new Error('PostgreSQL returned no JSON plan for the captured real BFF peer/owner display query.');
    logPlanDiagnostic('BFF-thread-peer-owner-display', threadDisplaySql.query, threadDisplayParams,
      threadPlanRows[0]?.['QUERY PLAN'], threadPlan);
    const peerOwnerRoutePlanNodes = flattenPlan(threadPlan);
    for (const table of ['conversation_routes', 'contact_identities', 'business_channel_identities']) {
      expect(peerOwnerRoutePlanNodes.some((node) => node['Relation Name'] === table)).toBe(true);
    }
    const threadRouteMain = peerOwnerRoutePlanNodes.filter((node) => node['Relation Name'] === 'conversation_routes' && node.Alias === 'r');
    const threadRouteCount = peerOwnerRoutePlanNodes.filter((node) => node['Relation Name'] === 'conversation_routes' && node.Alias === 'rr');
    const threadPeerScans = peerOwnerRoutePlanNodes.filter((node) => node['Relation Name'] === 'contact_identities' && node.Alias === 'ci');
    const threadOwnerScans = peerOwnerRoutePlanNodes.filter((node) => node['Relation Name'] === 'business_channel_identities' && node.Alias === 'b');
    expect(threadRouteMain.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(threadRouteCount.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(threadPeerScans.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(threadOwnerScans.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(threadDisplaySql.query).toMatch(/r\.conversation_id\s*=\s*cv\.id/i);
    expect(threadDisplaySql.query).toMatch(/r\.bu_id\s*=\s*cv\.bu_id/i);
    expect(threadDisplaySql.query).toMatch(/ci\.id\s*=\s*r\.contact_identity_id/i);
    expect(threadDisplaySql.query).toMatch(/ci\.bu_id\s*=\s*r\.bu_id/i);
    expect(threadDisplaySql.query).toMatch(/b\.id\s*=\s*r\.business_identity_id/i);
    expect(threadDisplaySql.query).toMatch(/b\.bu_id\s*=\s*r\.bu_id/i);
    expect(usesDeclaredIndex(threadPlan, 'conversation_routes', routeIndexDefinitions, 'r', /conversation_id/i)).toBe(true);
    expect(usesDeclaredIndex(threadPlan, 'conversation_routes', routeIndexDefinitions, 'rr', /conversation_id/i)).toBe(true);
    const primaryIdPeerIndex = usesDeclaredIndex(threadPlan, 'contact_identities', routeIndexes, 'ci', /(?:^|\W)id(?:\W|$)/i);
    const scopedContactBuPeerIndex = threadPeerScans.some((node) => {
      const indexName = node['Index Name'];
      const declaredIndex = routeIndexes.some((index) => index.tablename === 'contact_identities'
        && index.indexname === indexName && /\(contact_id, bu_id\)/i.test(index.indexdef));
      const indexCondition = node['Index Cond'] ?? '';
      const residualFilter = node.Filter ?? '';
      return node['Node Type'] === 'Index Scan' && declaredIndex
        && /contact_id\s*=\s*r\.contact_id/i.test(indexCondition)
        && /bu_id\s*=\s*r\.bu_id/i.test(indexCondition)
        && /id\s*=\s*r\.contact_identity_id/i.test(residualFilter)
        && /platform\s*=\s*r\.platform/i.test(residualFilter)
        && /scope_reference\s*=\s*r\.contact_scope_reference/i.test(residualFilter);
    });
    expect(primaryIdPeerIndex || scopedContactBuPeerIndex).toBe(true);
    expect(threadRouteMain.reduce((total, node) => total + scanWork(node), 0)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);
    expect(threadRouteCount.reduce((total, node) => total + scanWork(node), 0)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);
    expect(threadPeerScans.reduce((total, node) => total + scanWork(node), 0)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);
    const ownerRowsInFixture = await prisma.businessChannelIdentity.count({ where: { buId: { in: buIds } } });
    expect(threadOwnerScans.reduce((total, node) => total + scanWork(node), 0)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(ownerRowsInFixture);
    const peerOwnerLookupPlans = {
      source: 'actual full BFF thread consumer display query', capturedSql: threadDisplaySql.query,
      capturedParams: threadDisplayParams, plan: threadPlan,
      relevantNodes: peerOwnerRoutePlanNodes.filter((node) => ['conversation_routes', 'contact_identities',
        'business_channel_identities'].includes(node['Relation Name'] ?? '')).map(planNodeEvidence),
      mainRouteAccess: threadRouteMain.map(planNodeEvidence), lateralRouteCountAccess: threadRouteCount.map(planNodeEvidence),
      peerAccess: threadPeerScans.map(planNodeEvidence), ownerAccess: threadOwnerScans.map(planNodeEvidence), ownerRowsInFixture,
    };

    // The actual reply-options consumer must resolve one qualified route despite the twelve unrelated accounts.
    const expectedOptionRoute = await prisma.conversationRoute.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: rowsByBu[0]![0]!.routeId },
      select: { contactIdentityId: true, businessIdentityId: true } });
    expect(expectedOptionRoute).toEqual({ contactIdentityId: expect.any(String),
      businessIdentityId: rowsByBu[0]![0]!.account.businessIdentityId });
    queryEvents.length = 0;
    const optionsProbe = await bff(cookiesByBu[0]![0]!).get(`/api/bff/conversations/${rowsByBu[0]![0]!.conversationId}/reply-options`).expect(200);
    expect(optionsProbe.body.options).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(optionsProbe.body.options[0]).toMatchObject({ routeId: rowsByBu[0]![0]!.routeId,
      channelId: rowsByBu[0]![0]!.account.channelId, available: true, windowOpen: true, reason: 'AVAILABLE' });
    const optionsRouteQuery = queryEvents.find(({ query }) => /\b(?:FROM|JOIN)\s+(?:"public"\.)?"?conversation_routes"?/i.test(query));
    const optionsPeerQuery = queryEvents.find(({ query }) => /\b(?:FROM|JOIN)\s+(?:"public"\.)?"?contact_identities"?/i.test(query));
    const optionsOwnerQuery = queryEvents.find(({ query }) => /\b(?:FROM|JOIN)\s+(?:"public"\.)?"?business_channel_identities"?/i.test(query));
    if (!optionsRouteQuery || !optionsPeerQuery || !optionsOwnerQuery) {
      throw new Error('Actual reply-options consumer did not emit its route, peer and business-owner lookup SQL.');
    }
    const optionsRouteParams = JSON.parse(optionsRouteQuery.params) as unknown[];
    expect(JSON.stringify(optionsRouteParams)).toContain(rowsByBu[0]![0]!.conversationId);
    expect(JSON.stringify(optionsRouteParams)).toContain(rowsByBu[0]![0]!.buId);
    const optionsPeerParams = JSON.parse(optionsPeerQuery.params) as unknown[];
    const optionsOwnerParams = JSON.parse(optionsOwnerQuery.params) as unknown[];
    expect(JSON.stringify(optionsPeerParams)).toContain(expectedOptionRoute.contactIdentityId);
    expect(JSON.stringify(optionsOwnerParams)).toContain(expectedOptionRoute.businessIdentityId);
    const qualifiedWhere = (query: string) => query.match(/\bWHERE\b([\s\S]*)/i)?.[1] ?? '';
    expect(qualifiedWhere(optionsRouteQuery.query)).toMatch(/conversation_id[\s\S]*(?:=|\bIN\b)/i);
    expect(qualifiedWhere(optionsRouteQuery.query)).toMatch(/bu_id[\s\S]*(?:=|\bIN\b)/i);
    expect(qualifiedWhere(optionsPeerQuery.query)).toMatch(/(?:contact_identity_id|"id")[\s\S]*(?:=|\bIN\b)/i);
    expect(qualifiedWhere(optionsOwnerQuery.query)).toMatch(/(?:business_identity_id|"id")[\s\S]*(?:=|\bIN\b)/i);
    const optionsLookupCandidates = [optionsRouteQuery, optionsPeerQuery, optionsOwnerQuery];
    const optionsLookupEvents = optionsLookupCandidates.filter((event, index, events) =>
      events.findIndex((candidate) => candidate.query === event.query) === index);
    const optionsLookupPlans: Array<{ table: 'route' | 'peer' | 'owner'; capturedSql: string;
      capturedParams: unknown[]; plan: PlanNode }> = [];
    for (const event of optionsLookupEvents) {
      const params = JSON.parse(event.params) as unknown[];
      const planRows = await prisma.$queryRawUnsafe<Array<{ 'QUERY PLAN': Array<{ Plan: PlanNode }> }>>(
        `EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS, FORMAT JSON) ${event.query}`, ...params);
      const plan = planRows[0]?.['QUERY PLAN']?.[0]?.Plan;
      if (!plan) throw new Error('PostgreSQL returned no JSON plan for an actual reply-options route/peer/owner query.');
      const table = /conversation_routes/i.test(event.query) ? 'route'
        : /contact_identities/i.test(event.query) ? 'peer' : 'owner';
      logPlanDiagnostic(`BFF-reply-options-${table}`, event.query, params, planRows[0]?.['QUERY PLAN'], plan);
      optionsLookupPlans.push({ table, capturedSql: event.query, capturedParams: params, plan });
    }
    const boundReplyOptionIds = JSON.stringify(optionsLookupPlans.map(({ capturedParams }) => capturedParams));
    expect(boundReplyOptionIds).toContain(rowsByBu[0]![0]!.conversationId);
    expect(boundReplyOptionIds).toContain(rowsByBu[0]![0]!.buId);
    expect(optionsLookupPlans.some(({ capturedSql }) => /contact_identity_id/i.test(capturedSql))).toBe(true);
    expect(optionsLookupPlans.some(({ capturedSql }) => /business_identity_id/i.test(capturedSql))).toBe(true);
    expect(optionsLookupPlans.some(({ capturedSql }) => /conversation_id/i.test(capturedSql))).toBe(true);
    expect(optionsLookupPlans.some(({ capturedSql }) => /bu_id/i.test(capturedSql))).toBe(true);
    const optionsPlanEvidence = optionsLookupPlans.map(({ table, ...lookup }) => {
      const relevantRelation = table === 'route' ? 'conversation_routes'
        : table === 'peer' ? 'contact_identities' : 'business_channel_identities';
      const relationScans = flattenPlan(lookup.plan).filter((node) => node['Relation Name'] === relevantRelation);
      expect(relationScans.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      expect(relationScans.reduce((total, node) => total + scanWork(node), 0))
        .toBeLessThanOrEqual(table === 'owner' ? ownerRowsInFixture : 1);
      if (table !== 'owner') expect(usesDeclaredIndex(lookup.plan, relevantRelation, routeIndexes)).toBe(true);
      return { table, ...lookup, relevantScans: relationScans.map(planNodeEvidence) };
    });

    // Five rounds of twenty real BFF clients per class, after five measured warmups.
    // Query budgets are checked against isolated single-request event slices above each concurrent profile;
    // concurrent aggregate query counts are emitted as-is and are never divided by twenty.
    const requestProfile = async (kind: 'lookup' | 'options' | 'list1' | 'list2', row: CohortRow) => {
      const cookie = cookiesByBu[buIds.indexOf(row.buId)]![0]!;
      if (kind === 'lookup') return request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/bff/conversations/${row.conversationId}/messages`)
        .query({ limite: 50, marcarLida: false }).set('cookie', cookie).then((response) => response);
      if (kind === 'options') return bff(cookie).get(`/api/bff/conversations/${row.conversationId}/reply-options`).then((response) => response);
      const page = kind === 'list1' ? 1 : 2;
      return bff(cookie).get(`/api/bff/business-units/${row.buId}/conversations`).query({ pagina: page, limite: 50 })
        .then((response) => response);
    };
    const sampleRowsByBu = accountBalancedSampleRows();
    const sampleRows = sampleRowsByBu.flat();
    const rowByConversationId = new Map(rowsByBu.flat().map((row) => [row.conversationId, row]));
    const assertPageDisplays = (conversations: ConsumerResponseBody['conversations']) => {
      for (const conversation of conversations) {
        const row = rowByConversationId.get(conversation.id);
        expect(row).toBeDefined();
        expect(conversation.display).toMatchObject({ platform: 'INSTAGRAM', instagramUsername: row!.account.username });
      }
    };
    const expectedPages = new Map<string, { page1: string[]; page2: string[] }>();
    for (const buId of buIds) {
      const [page1, page2] = await Promise.all([orderedPageIds(buId, 1), orderedPageIds(buId, 2)]);
      expect(page1).toHaveLength(50);
      expect(page2).toHaveLength(50);
      expect(page1.some(({ id }) => page2.some((next) => next.id === id))).toBe(false);
      expectedPages.set(buId, { page1: page1.map(({ id }) => id), page2: page2.map(({ id }) => id) });
    }
    const isolatedDomainQueryCounts: Record<'lookup' | 'options' | 'list1' | 'list2', number[]> = {
      lookup: [], options: [], list1: [], list2: [],
    };
    for (const kind of ['lookup', 'options', 'list1', 'list2'] as const) for (const row of sampleRows) {
      queryEvents.length = 0;
      const isolated = await measureRequest(() => requestProfile(kind, row));
      if (kind === 'list1' || kind === 'list2') expect(isolated.domainQueries).toBeLessThanOrEqual(8);
      isolatedDomainQueryCounts[kind].push(isolated.domainQueries);
      if (kind === 'lookup') {
        expect(isolated.response.body.conversa.id).toBe(row.conversationId);
        expect(isolated.response.body.conversa.display).toMatchObject({ platform: 'INSTAGRAM', instagramUsername: row.account.username });
        expect(isolated.response.body.messages.some((message: { direction: string; channelOrigin: string }) => message.direction === 'IN' && message.channelOrigin === 'INSTAGRAM')).toBe(true);
        expect(isolated.response.body.paginacao.limite).toBe(50);
      } else if (kind === 'options') {
        expect(isolated.response.body.schemaVersion).toBe(2);
        expect(isolated.response.body.options).toHaveLength(1);
        expect(isolated.response.body.options[0]).toMatchObject({ routeId: row.routeId, channelId: row.account.channelId,
          generation: expect.any(Number), contextFingerprint: expect.stringMatching(/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/i),
          available: true, windowOpen: true, reason: 'AVAILABLE' });
      } else {
        expect(isolated.response.body).toMatchObject({ total: 10_000, pagina: kind === 'list1' ? 1 : 2, temMais: true });
        expect(isolated.response.body.conversations).toHaveLength(50);
        expect(isolated.response.body.conversations.map((conversation: { id: string }) => conversation.id))
          .toEqual(expectedPages.get(row.buId)![kind === 'list1' ? 'page1' : 'page2']);
        assertPageDisplays(isolated.response.body.conversations);
      }
    }
    const foreign = await bff(cookiesByBu[1]![0]!).get(`/api/bff/conversations/${rowsByBu[0]![0]!.conversationId}/messages`)
      .query({ limite: 50, marcarLida: false });
    expect(foreign.status).toBe(404);
    expect((await bff(cookiesByBu[1]![0]!).get(`/api/bff/conversations/${rowsByBu[0]![0]!.conversationId}/reply-options`)).status).toBe(404);
    expect((await bff(cookiesByBu[1]![0]!).get(`/api/bff/business-units/${buIds[0]}/conversations`)
      .query({ pagina: 1, limite: 50 })).status).toBe(404);
    const samples: Record<'lookup' | 'options' | 'list1' | 'list2', number[]> = { lookup: [], options: [], list1: [], list2: [] };
    for (const kind of ['lookup', 'options', 'list1', 'list2'] as const) {
      for (let warm = 0; warm < 5; warm += 1) {
        const row = sampleRows[warm]!;
        const response = await requestProfile(kind, row);
        expect(response.status).toBe(200);
      }
    }
    let aggregateQueries = 0;
    for (let round = 0; round < 5; round += 1) {
      for (const kind of ['lookup', 'options', 'list1', 'list2'] as const) {
        const startEvent = queryEvents.length;
        const jobs = Array.from({ length: 20 }, (_, client) => {
          const row = sampleRows[(round * 20 + client) % sampleRows.length]!;
          const started = performance.now();
          return requestProfile(kind, row).then((response) => ({ response, elapsedMs: performance.now() - started, row }));
        });
        const results = await Promise.all(jobs);
        aggregateQueries += queryEvents.slice(startEvent).filter(({ query }) => domainQuery(query)).length;
        for (const result of results) {
          expect(result.response.status).toBe(200);
          samples[kind].push(result.elapsedMs);
          if (kind === 'lookup') {
            expect(result.response.body.conversa.id).toBe(result.row.conversationId);
            expect(result.response.body.conversa.display).toMatchObject({ platform: 'INSTAGRAM', instagramUsername: result.row.account.username });
            expect(result.response.body.messages.some((message: { direction: string; channelOrigin: string }) => message.direction === 'IN' && message.channelOrigin === 'INSTAGRAM')).toBe(true);
            expect(result.response.body.paginacao.limite).toBe(50);
          } else if (kind === 'options') {
            expect(result.response.body.schemaVersion).toBe(2);
            expect(result.response.body.options).toHaveLength(1);
            expect(result.response.body.options[0]).toMatchObject({ routeId: result.row.routeId,
              channelId: result.row.account.channelId, generation: expect.any(Number),
              contextFingerprint: expect.stringMatching(/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/i),
              available: true, windowOpen: true, reason: 'AVAILABLE' });
          } else {
            const expectedPage = kind === 'list1' ? 1 : 2;
            expect(result.response.body).toMatchObject({ total: 10_000, pagina: expectedPage, temMais: true });
            expect(result.response.body.conversations).toHaveLength(50);
            expect(result.response.body.conversations.map((conversation: { id: string }) => conversation.id))
              .toEqual(expectedPages.get(result.row.buId)![expectedPage === 1 ? 'page1' : 'page2']);
            assertPageDisplays(result.response.body.conversations);
          }
        }
      }
    }
    const databaseResourcesAfterEps = await databaseResourceSnapshot();
    const processCpuDelta = process.cpuUsage(processCpuBeforeEps);
    const processMemoryAfterEps = process.memoryUsage();
    const resourceWindowElapsedMs = performance.now() - resourceWindowStartedMonotonicMs;
    const epsResourceEvidence = {
      scope: 'Own Vitest test-worker process and the current owned synthetic validation database; samples bracket this EPS case, outside individual request timers and isolated query-count slices.',
      sampledAt: { started: resourceWindowStartedAt, ended: new Date().toISOString(), elapsedMs: resourceWindowElapsedMs },
      process: { cpuUsageDeltaMicroseconds: processCpuDelta, memoryBeforeBytes: processMemoryBeforeEps,
        memoryAfterBytes: processMemoryAfterEps,
        memoryDeltaBytes: { rss: processMemoryAfterEps.rss - processMemoryBeforeEps.rss,
          heapTotal: processMemoryAfterEps.heapTotal - processMemoryBeforeEps.heapTotal,
          heapUsed: processMemoryAfterEps.heapUsed - processMemoryBeforeEps.heapUsed,
          external: processMemoryAfterEps.external - processMemoryBeforeEps.external,
          arrayBuffers: processMemoryAfterEps.arrayBuffers - processMemoryBeforeEps.arrayBuffers } },
      database: { scope: 'current_database() cumulative pg_stat_database deltas over the bracketed interval; counter updates are asynchronous, may include concurrent clients, and are not per-request attribution. Activity and database size are endpoint snapshots, not peaks.',
        before: databaseResourcesBeforeEps, after: databaseResourcesAfterEps,
        cumulativeCounterDeltas: {
          committedTransactions: databaseResourcesAfterEps.cumulativeCounters.committedTransactions - databaseResourcesBeforeEps.cumulativeCounters.committedTransactions,
          rolledBackTransactions: databaseResourcesAfterEps.cumulativeCounters.rolledBackTransactions - databaseResourcesBeforeEps.cumulativeCounters.rolledBackTransactions,
          blocksRead: databaseResourcesAfterEps.cumulativeCounters.blocksRead - databaseResourcesBeforeEps.cumulativeCounters.blocksRead,
          blocksHit: databaseResourcesAfterEps.cumulativeCounters.blocksHit - databaseResourcesBeforeEps.cumulativeCounters.blocksHit,
          tuplesReturned: databaseResourcesAfterEps.cumulativeCounters.tuplesReturned - databaseResourcesBeforeEps.cumulativeCounters.tuplesReturned,
          tuplesFetched: databaseResourcesAfterEps.cumulativeCounters.tuplesFetched - databaseResourcesBeforeEps.cumulativeCounters.tuplesFetched,
          tuplesInserted: databaseResourcesAfterEps.cumulativeCounters.tuplesInserted - databaseResourcesBeforeEps.cumulativeCounters.tuplesInserted,
          tuplesUpdated: databaseResourcesAfterEps.cumulativeCounters.tuplesUpdated - databaseResourcesBeforeEps.cumulativeCounters.tuplesUpdated,
          tuplesDeleted: databaseResourcesAfterEps.cumulativeCounters.tuplesDeleted - databaseResourcesBeforeEps.cumulativeCounters.tuplesDeleted,
          temporaryFiles: databaseResourcesAfterEps.cumulativeCounters.temporaryFiles - databaseResourcesBeforeEps.cumulativeCounters.temporaryFiles,
          temporaryBytes: databaseResourcesAfterEps.cumulativeCounters.temporaryBytes - databaseResourcesBeforeEps.cumulativeCounters.temporaryBytes,
          deadlocks: databaseResourcesAfterEps.cumulativeCounters.deadlocks - databaseResourcesBeforeEps.cumulativeCounters.deadlocks,
          conflicts: databaseResourcesAfterEps.cumulativeCounters.conflicts - databaseResourcesBeforeEps.cumulativeCounters.conflicts,
        } },
    };
    const eps = {
      workload: 'CP5 EPS actual BFF ID lookup, V2 reply-options, and list page 1/page 2',
      cohort: { tenants: 2, businessUnits: 2, conversationsPerBu: [10_000, 10_000], instagramAccountsPerBu: [12, 12],
        waBusinessResourcesPerBu: 1, waAdaptersPerResource: ['OFICIAL', 'NAO_OFICIAL'] },
      samplesPerClass: 100, warmupRequestsPerClass: 5, rounds: 5, clientsPerRound: 20,
      p95Ms: { lookup: percentile95(samples.lookup), replyOptions: percentile95(samples.options), listPage1: percentile95(samples.list1), listPage2: percentile95(samples.list2) },
      thresholdsMs: { lookup: 1_000, replyOptions: 1_000, list50: 2_000 },
      aggregateDomainQueriesAcrossConcurrentSamples: aggregateQueries,
      actualExternalProviderHttpCalls: providerCounters().instagram - providerCallsBeforeEps.instagram,
      metaProviderCallsDuringEps: providerCounters().meta - providerCallsBeforeEps.meta,
      evolutionProviderCallsDuringEps: providerCounters().evolution - providerCallsBeforeEps.evolution,
      routePlan: { source: 'actual full BFF conversation-list display query', capturedSql: routeSql.query,
        capturedParams: params, plan: planRows[0]?.['QUERY PLAN'], identityOwnerRoutePlans,
        mainRouteAlias: 'r', mainRouteAccess: mainRouteScans.map(planNodeEvidence),
        lateralRouteCountAlias: 'rr', lateralRouteCountAccess: lateralRouteScans.map(planNodeEvidence),
        peerAlias: 'ci', peerAccess: listPeerScans.map(planNodeEvidence),
        indexDefinitions: routeIndexes },
      peerOwnerLookupPlans,
      replyOptionsRouteLookup: { source: 'actual full BFF reply-options consumer Prisma route/peer/owner queries',
        plans: optionsPlanEvidence },
      runtime: { cpuCount: cpus().length, totalMemoryBytes: totalmem(), nodeVersion: process.version,
        cpuModel: cpus()[0]?.model ?? 'unreported', prismaVersion: Prisma.prismaVersion.client,
        postgresImageDeclared: 'postgres:16 (validation-postgres in docker-compose.channels.yml)',
        postgres: (await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ version: string; server_version: string }>>`SELECT version(), current_setting('server_version') AS server_version`)[0] },
      resourceMeasurements: epsResourceEvidence,
      latenciesMs: samples,
      isolatedDomainQueryCountsPerPage: isolatedDomainQueryCounts,
      cohortRetained: cohortReady,
    };
    console.info(`[CP5-EPS-ACTUAL]${JSON.stringify(eps)}`);
    expect(eps.p95Ms.lookup).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1_000);
    expect(eps.p95Ms.replyOptions).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1_000);
    expect(eps.p95Ms.listPage1).toBeLessThanOrEqual(2_000);
    expect(eps.p95Ms.listPage2).toBeLessThanOrEqual(2_000);
    expectProviderCountersUnchanged(providerCallsBeforeEps);
    await assertRetainedCohort();
  }, SETUP_TIMEOUT_MS);

  it('enforces Instagram outbound UTF-8 byte limits through the real BFF before admission and provider POST', async () => {
    await assertRetainedCohort();
    const target = rowsByBu[0]![0]!;
    const cookie = cookiesByBu[0]![0]!;
    const optionsResponse = await bff(cookie).get(`/api/bff/conversations/${target.conversationId}/reply-options`).expect(200);
    expect(optionsResponse.body.options).toHaveLength(1);
    const option = optionsResponse.body.options[0];
    expect(option).toMatchObject({ routeId: target.routeId, channelId: target.account.channelId,
      channelOrigin: 'INSTAGRAM', available: true, requiresWindow: true, windowOpen: true,
      textConstraint: { unit: 'UTF8_BYTES', max: 1000 } });
    const selectedRoute = await prisma.conversationRoute.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: target.routeId },
      select: { contactIdentityId: true, businessIdentityId: true } });
    const requestBody = (text: string) => ({ schemaVersion: 2, routeId: option.routeId, channelId: option.channelId,
      expectedGeneration: option.generation, contextFingerprint: option.contextFingerprint, texto: text });
    const effectSnapshot = async () => {
      const [messages, admissions] = await Promise.all([
        prisma.message.count({ where: { conversationId: target.conversationId } }),
        prisma.dispatchAdmission.count({ where: { conversationId: target.conversationId } }),
      ]);
      return { messages, admissions, providerCounters: providerCounters(), providerPosts: instagramFake.messages.length };
    };

    const validCases = [
      { label: 'ASCII', text: 'a'.repeat(1000) },
      { label: 'accented', text: 'é'.repeat(500) },
      { label: 'supplementary emoji', text: '🙂'.repeat(250) },
    ];
    for (const boundary of validCases) {
      expect(Buffer.byteLength(boundary.text, 'utf8')).toBe(1000);
      const before = await effectSnapshot();
      const response = await bff(cookie).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${target.conversationId}/messages`)
        .send(requestBody(boundary.text)).expect(201);
      const accepted = await prisma.message.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { id: response.body.id, conversationId: target.conversationId,
        direction: 'OUT', channelOrigin: 'INSTAGRAM' } });
      expect(accepted.content).toBe(boundary.text);
      expect(instagramFake.messages).toHaveLength(before.providerPosts + 1);
      expect(instagramFake.messages.at(-1)).toEqual({ accountId: target.account.accountId, recipientId: target.peerId, text: boundary.text });
      expect(instagramFake.calls.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ method: 'POST',
        url: `https://graph.instagram.com/v26.0/${target.account.accountId}/messages` });
      expect(providerCounters()).toEqual({ ...before.providerCounters, instagram: before.providerCounters.instagram + 1 });
      expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { id: response.body.id, content: boundary.text, conversationId: target.conversationId } })).toBe(1);
