      expect((await prisma.conversationRoute.count({ where: { id: target.routeId, conversationId: target.conversationId,
        contactIdentityId: selectedRoute.contactIdentityId, businessIdentityId: selectedRoute.businessIdentityId, platform: 'INSTAGRAM' } }))).toBe(1);
    }

    const invalidCases = [
      { label: 'ASCII', text: 'a'.repeat(1001), bytes: 1001 },
      { label: 'accented', text: 'é'.repeat(501), bytes: 1002 },
      { label: 'supplementary emoji', text: '🙂'.repeat(251), bytes: 1004 },
    ];
    for (const boundary of invalidCases) {
      expect(Buffer.byteLength(boundary.text, 'utf8')).toBe(boundary.bytes);
      const before = await effectSnapshot();
      const response = await bff(cookie).post(`/api/bff/conversations/${target.conversationId}/messages`)
        .send(requestBody(boundary.text));
      expect(response.status).toBe(400);
      expect(response.body).toMatchObject({ code: 'TEXTO_LONGO' });
      expect(await effectSnapshot()).toEqual(before);
    }
    await assertRetainedCohort();
  }, SETUP_TIMEOUT_MS);

  it('proves five OAuth reservation and confirmation contention waves', async () => {
    await assertRetainedCohort();
    // Actor 0 has four initial account authorizations plus five fresh, genuine attempts: 9/hour and 17/BU.
    // Each wave has twenty in-flight identical reservation requests, one callback and one confirmation.
    const oauthWaveResults: Array<{ wave: number; reservationRequests: number; durableAttempts: number; subscriptionPosts: number; statuses: number[] }> = [];
    const waveShortTokens: string[] = [];
    const waveAccessTokens: string[] = [];
    const waveAuthorizationReferences: string[] = [];
    const waveProfessionalIds: string[] = [];
    const ownerCookie = cookiesByBu[0]![0]!;
    const owner = accountsByBu[0]![0]!;
    for (let wave = 0; wave < 5; wave += 1) {
      const idempotencyKey = randomUUID();
      const path = `/api/bff/business-units/${owner.buId}/instagram-authorizations`;
      const beforeAttempts = await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.count({ where: { userId: owner.actorId, buId: owner.buId } });
      const beforePosts = instagramFake.calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps')).length;
      const waveShortToken = `synthetic-cp5-wave-short-${wave}-${randomUUID()}`;
      const waveAccessToken = `synthetic-cp5-wave-long-${wave}-${randomUUID()}`;
      const waveAuthorizationReference = `synthetic-cp5-wave-authref-${wave}-${randomUUID()}`;
      waveShortTokens.push(waveShortToken);
      waveAccessTokens.push(waveAccessToken);
      waveAuthorizationReferences.push(waveAuthorizationReference);
      instagramFake.shortEnvelope = { data: [{ access_token: waveShortToken, user_id: waveAuthorizationReference,
        permissions: 'instagram_business_basic,instagram_business_manage_messages' }] };
      instagramFake.longEnvelope = { access_token: waveAccessToken, expires_in: 7200 };
      const waveUsername = `cp5_wave_${wave}`;
      instagramFake.meEnvelope = { user_id: `synthetic-cp5-oauth-wave-${wave}-${randomUUID()}`, username: waveUsername };
      instagramFake.subscribed = false;
      const creates = await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: 20 }, () => bff(ownerCookie).post(path)
        .set('idempotency-key', idempotencyKey).send({ operation: 'CONNECT' }).then((response) => response)));
      expect(creates.map(({ status }) => status).every((status) => status === 201 || status === 200)).toBe(true);
      const body = creates[0]!.body;
      expect(creates.every(({ body: candidate }) => candidate.id === body.id && candidate.authorizationUrl === body.authorizationUrl)).toBe(true);
      expect(await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.count({ where: { id: body.id, userId: owner.actorId, buId: owner.buId } })).toBe(1);
      expect(await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.count({ where: { userId: owner.actorId, buId: owner.buId } })).toBe(beforeAttempts + 1);
      const mismatch = await bff(ownerCookie).post(path).set('idempotency-key', idempotencyKey)
        .send({ operation: 'REAUTHORIZE', channelId: owner.channelId, expectedGeneration: 1 });
      expect(mismatch.status).toBe(409);
      const callbackState = new URL(body.authorizationUrl).searchParams.get('state');
      if (!callbackState) throw new Error(`OAuth wave ${wave} reservation omitted state.`);
      const tokenCallsBefore = instagramFake.calls.filter((call) => /\/oauth\/access_token|\/access_token|\/v26\.0\/me$/.test(new URL(call.url).pathname)).length;
      await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/callback').set('cookie', ownerCookie)
        .query({ state: callbackState, code: `synthetic-cp5-oauth-wave-code-${wave}-${randomUUID()}` }).expect(303);
      const exchangeCalls = instagramFake.calls.slice(-3);
      expect(exchangeCalls.map(({ method, url }) => [method, new URL(url).pathname])).toEqual([
        ['POST', '/oauth/access_token'], ['GET', '/access_token'], ['GET', '/v26.0/me'],
      ]);
      expect(instagramFake.calls.filter((call) => /\/oauth\/access_token|\/access_token|\/v26\.0\/me$/.test(new URL(call.url).pathname)).length)
        .toBe(tokenCallsBefore + 3);
      const confirmationKey = randomUUID();
      let releaseSubscription!: () => void;
      let enterSubscription!: () => void;
      const subscriptionHeld = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { releaseSubscription = resolve; });
      const subscriptionEntered = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { enterSubscription = resolve; });
      instagramFake.afterRequestHook = async (method, url) => {
        if (method === 'POST' && url.pathname.endsWith('/subscribed_apps')) {
          enterSubscription();
          await subscriptionHeld;
        }
      };
      const settled: Array<{ status: number; body: { code?: string } }> = [];
      const confirmationPromises = Array.from({ length: 20 }, () => bff(ownerCookie)
        .post(`/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${body.id}/confirm`).set('idempotency-key', confirmationKey).send({})
        .then((response) => { settled.push(response); return response; }));
      const beforeConfirmationPosts = instagramFake.calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps')).length;
      try {
        await waitFor(subscriptionEntered, `OAuth wave ${wave} real subscription HTTP barrier`);
        const firstPostCount = instagramFake.calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps')).length;
        expect(firstPostCount).toBe(beforeConfirmationPosts + 1);
        await waitFor((async () => {
          while (settled.length < 19) await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 10));
          return settled.slice();
        })(), `OAuth wave ${wave} nineteen losing confirmations`);
        expect(settled).toHaveLength(19);
        expect(settled.every(({ status, body: result }) => status === 409 && result.code === 'CONFIRMATION_IN_PROGRESS')).toBe(true);
      } finally {
        releaseSubscription();
        instagramFake.afterRequestHook = null;
        await Promise.allSettled(confirmationPromises);
      }
      const confirmations = await Promise.all(confirmationPromises);
      expect(confirmations.filter(({ status }) => status === 200)).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(confirmations.filter(({ status, body: result }) => status === 409 && result.code === 'CONFIRMATION_IN_PROGRESS')).toHaveLength(19);
      const attempt = await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: body.id } });
      expect(attempt).toMatchObject({ confirmationState: 'COMPLETED', subscriptionRequests: 1 });
      expect(instagramFake.calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps'))).toHaveLength(beforePosts + 1);
      if (!attempt.channelId || !attempt.professionalUserId) throw new Error('OAuth confirmation did not persist the selected channel and professional account.');
      expect(attempt.authorizationReference).toBe(waveAuthorizationReference);
      expect(attempt.professionalUserId).toBe(instagramFake.meEnvelope && (instagramFake.meEnvelope as { user_id: string }).user_id);
      waveProfessionalIds.push(attempt.professionalUserId);
      expect(await prisma.buChannel.count({ where: { id: attempt.channelId, buId: owner.buId, type: 'INSTAGRAM' } })).toBe(1);
      expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.count({ where: { provider: 'INSTAGRAM', externalResourceId: attempt.professionalUserId,
        buId: owner.buId, currentChannelId: attempt.channelId } })).toBe(1);
      expect(await new ChannelCredentialResolver(prisma).resolve(attempt.channelId, 'verify'))
        .toMatchObject({ instagramAccountId: attempt.professionalUserId, accessToken: waveAccessToken });
      const replay = await bff(ownerCookie).post(`/api/bff/instagram-authorizations/${body.id}/confirm`)
        .set('idempotency-key', confirmationKey).send({});
      expect(replay.status).toBe(200);
      expect(instagramFake.calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'POST' && call.url.endsWith('/subscribed_apps'))).toHaveLength(beforePosts + 1);
      oauthWaveResults.push({ wave, reservationRequests: creates.length, durableAttempts: 1, subscriptionPosts: 1,
        statuses: confirmations.map(({ status }) => status) });
    }
    const ownerAttempts = await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.findMany({ where: { userId: owner.actorId, buId: owner.buId }, orderBy: { createdAt: 'asc' } });
    expect(ownerAttempts.length).toBe(9);
    expect(await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.count({ where: { buId: owner.buId } })).toBe(17);
    expect(new Set(waveShortTokens).size).toBe(5);
    expect(new Set(waveAccessTokens).size).toBe(5);
    expect(new Set(waveAuthorizationReferences).size).toBe(5);
    expect(new Set(waveProfessionalIds).size).toBe(5);
    console.info(`[CP5-OAUTH-ACTUAL]${JSON.stringify({ waves: oauthWaveResults, perActor: ownerAttempts.length,
      perBu: await prisma.instagramAuthorizationAttempt.count({ where: { buId: owner.buId } }),
      initialSendProofReuse: 'api-app/test/initial-message.e2e-spec.ts existing five waves x twenty; reuse requires exact current hash and full current canonical ALL',
      ordinaryConcurrencyProof: 'existing ordinary-to-ordinary single-use and initial/ordinary exclusion tests; no global ordinary lock or automatic retry' })}`);
    await assertRetainedCohort();
  }, SETUP_TIMEOUT_MS);

  it('deduplicates twenty concurrent signed replays and rejects a bad signature', async () => {
    await assertRetainedCohort();
    const providerCallsBeforeReplay = providerCounters();
    // Twenty concurrent signed replays of one event persist once and increment unread once.
    const replayTarget = rowsByBu[0]![1]!;
    const replayId = `synthetic-cp5-same-event-${randomUUID()}`;
    const replayBody = JSON.stringify({ object: 'instagram', entry: [{ id: replayTarget.account.accountId, messaging: [{
      sender: { id: replayTarget.peerId }, recipient: { id: replayTarget.account.accountId }, timestamp: Date.now(),
      message: { mid: replayId, text: 'Exactly one signed event across twenty concurrent retries.' },
    }] }] });
    const replaySig = signatureFor(replayBody);
    const signed = () => request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/instagram').set('content-type', 'application/json')
      .set('x-hub-signature-256', replaySig).send(replayBody);
    const beforeReplay = await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: replayTarget.conversationId }, select: { unreadCount: true } });
    const replayResults = await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: 20 }, signed));
    expect(replayResults.every(({ status }) => status === 200)).toBe(true);
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { externalId: replayId, buId: replayTarget.buId,
      conversationId: replayTarget.conversationId, direction: 'IN', channelOrigin: 'INSTAGRAM',
      providerResourceIdentityId: replayTarget.account.resourceIdentityId } })).toBe(1);
    expect(await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: replayTarget.conversationId }, select: { unreadCount: true } }))
      .toEqual({ unreadCount: beforeReplay.unreadCount + 1 });
    const afterReplay = await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: replayTarget.conversationId }, select: { unreadCount: true } });
    const wrongSignature = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/instagram').set('content-type', 'application/json')
      .set('x-hub-signature-256', 'sha256=00').send(replayBody);
    expect(wrongSignature.status).toBe(401);
    expect(await prisma.conversation.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: replayTarget.conversationId }, select: { unreadCount: true } })).toEqual(afterReplay);
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { externalId: replayId } })).toBe(1);
    expectProviderCountersUnchanged(providerCallsBeforeReplay);
    console.info(`[CP5-REPLAY-ACTUAL]${JSON.stringify({ requests: replayResults.length, persistedMessages: 1,
      unreadBefore: beforeReplay.unreadCount, unreadAfter: afterReplay.unreadCount, invalidSignatureStatus: wrongSignature.status })}`);
    await assertRetainedCohort();
  }, SETUP_TIMEOUT_MS);

  it('settles twenty cross-BU WhatsApp resource claims through one PostgreSQL owner', async () => {
    await assertRetainedCohort();
    // 20 real PostgreSQL claim transactions contend on one observed provider-proved phone.
    const sharedPhone = '5511997766554';
    const candidateBuIds: string[] = [];
    const candidates: Array<{ buId: string; channelId: string; providerIdentityId: string; adapter: 'OFICIAL' | 'NAO_OFICIAL'; externalId: string }> = [];
    for (let index = 0; index < 20; index += 1) {
      const ownerUnit = await prisma.businessUnit.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: buIds[index % 2]! }, select: { mantenedoraId: true, setorId: true } });
      const bu = await prisma.businessUnit.create({ data: { mantenedoraId: ownerUnit.mantenedoraId, setorId: ownerUnit.setorId,
        name: `CP5 isolated claim ${index} ${randomUUID()}` } });
      const adapter = index % 2 === 0 ? 'OFICIAL' as const : 'NAO_OFICIAL' as const;
      const externalId = `synthetic-cp5-resource-claim-${index}-${randomUUID()}`;
      const channel = await prisma.buChannel.create({ data: { buId: bu.id, type: adapter, status: 'CONECTANDO',
        ...(adapter === 'OFICIAL' ? { phoneNumberId: externalId, wabaId: `synthetic-cp5-claim-waba-${index}` }
          : { evolutionInstanceName: externalId }), ownNumberVerified: false, verifiedEpoch: null, providerConnectionState: 'PENDING' } });
      const provider = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.create({ data: { provider: adapter, externalResourceId: externalId,
        buId: bu.id, currentChannelId: channel.id, managedLifecycle: true, cleanupState: 'ACTIVE' } });
      candidateBuIds.push(bu.id);
      candidates.push({ buId: bu.id, channelId: channel.id, providerIdentityId: provider.id, adapter, externalId });
    }
    const actualPhoneObservations: Array<typeof candidates[number] & { provedPhone: string }> = [];
    // Query the fixed HTTP fakes one at a time: each official candidate scopes
    // the fake's verifier, so parallel mutation would make the observed proof
    // depend on scheduling rather than on the provider response.
    for (const [candidateIndex, candidate] of candidates.entries()) {
      if (candidate.adapter === 'OFICIAL') {
        const wabaId = `synthetic-cp5-claim-waba-${candidateIndex}`;
        metaFake.wabaRecursos = [...metaFake.wabaRecursos,
          { wabaId, phoneNumberId: candidate.externalId, telefone: sharedPhone }];
        const observed = await metaFake.verificarNumeroVinculado(wabaId, candidate.externalId, 'fixture-scoped-token');
        if (!observed) throw new Error('Fixed Meta HTTP fake did not return a provider-proved WhatsApp phone.');
        actualPhoneObservations.push({ ...candidate, provedPhone: observed.telefone });
        continue;
      }
      evolutionFake.inventory.set(candidate.externalId, { name: candidate.externalId, ownerJid: `${sharedPhone}@s.whatsapp.net`, connectionStatus: 'open' });
      const observed = await evolutionFake.consultarInstancia(candidate.externalId);
      if (observed?.ownerJid !== `${sharedPhone}@s.whatsapp.net`) throw new Error('Fixed Evolution HTTP fake did not return the common proved phone.');
      actualPhoneObservations.push({ ...candidate, provedPhone: observed.ownerJid.split('@')[0]! });
    }
    expect(actualPhoneObservations).toHaveLength(20);
    expect(new Set(actualPhoneObservations.map(({ buId }) => buId)).size).toBe(20);
    expect(new Set(actualPhoneObservations.map(({ externalId }) => externalId)).size).toBe(20);
    const providerCallsBeforeClaims = providerCounters();
    const claimDeadlocksBefore = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ deadlocks: bigint }>>`
      SELECT deadlocks FROM pg_stat_database WHERE datname = current_database()`;
    const ownerKey = JSON.stringify({ platform: 'WHATSAPP', namespace: 'WHATSAPP_RESOURCE', externalResourceId: sharedPhone });
    let releaseHolder!: () => void;
    let acquiredHolder!: () => void;
    const holderReleased = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { releaseHolder = resolve; });
    const holderAcquired = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { acquiredHolder = resolve; });
    let holderPid = 0;
    let holder: Promise<void> | undefined;
    let completedClaims = 0;
    let claims: Array<Promise<ClaimResult>> = [];
    let actualWaiters: number;
    let unsettledClaimsAtRelease: number;
    const pgObserver = new PrismaClient();
    try {
      await pgObserver.$connect();
      holder = prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
        const [pid] = await tx.$queryRaw<Array<{ pid: number }>>`SELECT pg_backend_pid() AS pid`;
        holderPid = pid.pid;
        await tx.$queryRaw`SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(${ownerKey}, 0))::text AS locked`;
        acquiredHolder();
        await holderReleased;
      }, { timeout: 90_000, maxWait: 20_000 });
      await waitFor(holderAcquired, 'native PostgreSQL resource-owner barrier');
      claims = actualPhoneObservations.map((candidate) => prisma.$transaction((tx) => claimWhatsAppBusinessResource(tx, {
        buId: candidate.buId, channelId: candidate.channelId, providerResourceIdentityId: candidate.providerIdentityId,
        adapter: candidate.adapter, provedPhone: candidate.provedPhone,
      }), { timeout: 90_000, maxWait: 20_000 }).then((value) => {
        completedClaims += 1; return { ok: true as const, value };
      }, (error: unknown) => { completedClaims += 1; return { ok: false as const, error }; }));
      actualWaiters = await waitFor((async () => {
        for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 150; attempt += 1) {
          const [row] = await pgObserver.$queryRaw<Array<{ blocked: number }>>`
            SELECT count(*)::int AS blocked FROM pg_stat_activity activity
            WHERE activity.pid <> ${holderPid} AND ${holderPid} = ANY(pg_blocking_pids(activity.pid))`;
          if (row.blocked >= 1) return row.blocked;
          await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 100));
        }
        throw new Error('No PostgreSQL contender was observed waiting on the real WhatsApp resource advisory lock.');
      })(), 'native PostgreSQL waiter observation', 20_000);
    } finally {
      unsettledClaimsAtRelease = claims.length - completedClaims;
      releaseHolder();
      await Promise.allSettled([...(holder ? [holder] : []), ...claims]);
      await pgObserver.$disconnect();
    }
    expect(actualWaiters).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(unsettledClaimsAtRelease).toBe(20);
    if (holder) await holder;
    const claimResults = await Promise.all(claims);
    const winners = claimResults.filter(({ ok }) => ok);
    const conflicts = claimResults.filter(({ ok }) => !ok);
    expect(winners).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(conflicts).toHaveLength(19);
    expect(conflicts.every(({ error }) => {
      const candidate = error as { response?: { code?: string } };
      return candidate?.response?.code === 'RESOURCE_OWNED';
    })).toBe(true);
    const committed = await prisma.businessChannelIdentity.findMany({ where: {
      platform: 'WHATSAPP', namespace: 'WHATSAPP_RESOURCE', externalResourceId: sharedPhone,
    } });
    expect(committed).toHaveLength(1);
    const winningCandidate = actualPhoneObservations[claimResults.findIndex(({ ok }) => ok)]!;
    expect(committed[0]).toMatchObject({ id: winners[0]!.value.id, buId: winningCandidate.buId,
      platform: 'WHATSAPP', namespace: 'WHATSAPP_RESOURCE', scopeReference: 'BU', externalResourceId: sharedPhone });
    expect(await prisma.buChannel.count({ where: { buId: { in: candidateBuIds }, businessIdentityId: { not: null } } })).toBe(1);
    expect(await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.count({ where: { buId: { in: candidateBuIds }, businessIdentityId: { not: null } } })).toBe(1);
    const claimDeadlocksAfter = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ deadlocks: bigint }>>`
      SELECT deadlocks FROM pg_stat_database WHERE datname = current_database()`;
    const claimDeadlockDelta = Number(claimDeadlocksAfter[0]?.deadlocks ?? 0n)
      - Number(claimDeadlocksBefore[0]?.deadlocks ?? 0n);
    expect(claimDeadlockDelta).toBe(0);
    expectProviderCountersUnchanged(providerCallsBeforeClaims);
    console.info(`[CP5-RESOURCE-CLAIMS-ACTUAL]${JSON.stringify({ contenders: 20, unsettledClaimsAtRelease,
      actualPostgresWaiters: actualWaiters, winners: winners.length, resourceOwnedConflicts: conflicts.length, claimDeadlockDelta })}`);
    await assertRetainedCohort();
  }, SETUP_TIMEOUT_MS);

  it('runs five same-cohort RLS rounds of twenty readers and twenty signed messages', async () => {
    await assertRetainedCohort();
    const providerCallsBeforeRls = providerCounters();
    // Five same-cohort RLS rounds, each with twenty real BFF readers and twenty full signed HTTP messages.
    const rlsRounds: Array<{ round: number; readers: number; messages: number; readDomainQueries: number; elapsedMs: number }> = [];
    const rlsStarted = performance.now();
    const rlsReadLatencies: number[] = [];
    const rlsReadDomainQueryCounts: number[] = [];
    const concurrentRlsReadLatencies: number[] = [];
    const readCookies = [cookiesByBu[0]![0]!, cookiesByBu[1]![0]!];
    const rlsRowsByBu = accountBalancedSampleRows();
    // Companion full-consumer requests record query counts and latency for
    // every exact RLS context without dividing a concurrent aggregate by twenty.
    for (let round = 0; round < 5; round += 1) for (let index = 0; index < 20; index += 1) {
      const row = rlsRowsByBu[index % 2]![round * 10 + Math.floor(index / 2)]!;
      const queryStart = queryEvents.length;
      const started = performance.now();
      const response = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/bff/conversations/${row.conversationId}/messages`)
        .query({ limite: 50, marcarLida: false }).set('cookie', readCookies[index % 2]!);
      rlsReadLatencies.push(performance.now() - started);
      expect(response.status).toBe(200);
      const ownEvents = queryEvents.slice(queryStart);
      const readDomainQueries = ownEvents.filter(({ query }) => domainQuery(query)).length;
      rlsReadDomainQueryCounts.push(readDomainQueries);
      expect(readDomainQueries).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      assertNoTimelineNPlusOne(ownEvents);
      expect(response.body.conversa.id).toBe(row.conversationId);
      expect(response.body.conversa.display).toMatchObject({ platform: 'INSTAGRAM', instagramUsername: row.account.username });
    }
    const initialDeadlocks = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ deadlocks: bigint }>>`
      SELECT deadlocks FROM pg_stat_database WHERE datname = current_database()`;
    for (let round = 0; round < 5; round += 1) {
      const startEvents = queryEvents.length;
      const rowsThisRound = Array.from({ length: 20 }, (_, index) => rlsRowsByBu[index % 2]![round * 10 + Math.floor(index / 2)]!);
      const unreadBefore = await prisma.conversation.findMany({ where: { id: { in: rowsThisRound.map(({ conversationId }) => conversationId) } },
        select: { id: true, unreadCount: true } });
      const unreadByConversation = new Map(unreadBefore.map(({ id, unreadCount }) => [id, unreadCount]));
      const beforeMessages = await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId: { in: buIds }, channelOrigin: 'INSTAGRAM', direction: 'IN' } });
      const eventIds = rowsThisRound.map((_, index) => `synthetic-cp5-rls-${round}-${index}-${randomUUID()}`);
      const readerJobs = Array.from({ length: 20 }, (_, index) => {
        const row = rowsThisRound[index]!;
        const started = performance.now();
        const cookie = readCookies[index % 2]!;
        return request(app.getHttpServer()).get(`/api/bff/conversations/${row.conversationId}/messages`)
          .query({ limite: 50, marcarLida: false }).set('cookie', cookie)
          .then((response) => ({ response, row, elapsedMs: performance.now() - started }));
      });
      const messageJobs = Array.from({ length: 20 }, (_, index) => {
        const row = rowsThisRound[index]!;
        const account = row.account;
        return signedBatch(account.accountId, [{ peer: row.peerId, id: eventIds[index]!,
          text: `Signed concurrent RLS message round ${round} item ${index}.` }]);
      });
      const [readResults, messageResults] = await Promise.all([Promise.all(readerJobs), Promise.all(messageJobs)]);
      expect(readResults.every(({ response }) => response.status === 200)).toBe(true);
      for (const { response, row, elapsedMs } of readResults) {
        concurrentRlsReadLatencies.push(elapsedMs);
        expect(response.body.conversa.id).toBe(row.conversationId);
        expect(response.body.conversa.display).toMatchObject({ platform: 'INSTAGRAM', instagramUsername: row.account.username });
      }
      expect(messageResults.every(({ status }) => status === 200)).toBe(true);
      const afterMessages = await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId: { in: buIds }, channelOrigin: 'INSTAGRAM', direction: 'IN' } });
      expect(afterMessages).toBe(beforeMessages + 20);
      const unreadAfter = await prisma.conversation.findMany({ where: { id: { in: rowsThisRound.map(({ conversationId }) => conversationId) } },
        select: { id: true, unreadCount: true } });
      expect(unreadAfter.every(({ id, unreadCount }) => unreadCount === unreadByConversation.get(id)! + 1)).toBe(true);
      for (let index = 0; index < rowsThisRound.length; index += 1) {
        const row = rowsThisRound[index]!;
        expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId: row.buId, conversationId: row.conversationId,
          externalId: eventIds[index], direction: 'IN', channelOrigin: 'INSTAGRAM',
          providerResourceIdentityId: row.account.resourceIdentityId } })).toBe(1);
        expect(await prisma.conversationRoute.count({ where: { id: row.routeId, buId: row.buId,
          conversationId: row.conversationId, businessIdentityId: row.account.businessIdentityId } })).toBe(1);
      }
      const roundQueries = queryEvents.slice(startEvents).filter(({ query }) => domainQuery(query)).length;
      rlsRounds.push({ round, readers: readResults.length, messages: messageResults.length, readDomainQueries: roundQueries,
        elapsedMs: performance.now() - rlsStarted });
      expect(await prisma.conversationRoute.count({ where: { buId: { in: buIds }, platform: 'INSTAGRAM' } })).toBe(20_000);
    }
    const pending = await prisma.$queryRaw<Array<{ blockers: bigint; deadlocks: bigint }>>`
      SELECT count(*) FILTER (WHERE cardinality(pg_blocking_pids(pid)) > 0) AS blockers,
        (SELECT deadlocks FROM pg_stat_database WHERE datname = current_database()) AS deadlocks
      FROM pg_stat_activity WHERE datname = current_database() AND pid <> pg_backend_pid()`;
    expect(Number(pending[0]?.blockers ?? 0n)).toBe(0);
    const finalDeadlocks = Number(pending[0]?.deadlocks ?? 0n);
    const deadlockDelta = finalDeadlocks - Number(initialDeadlocks[0]?.deadlocks ?? 0n);
    expect(deadlockDelta).toBe(0);
    expect(concurrentRlsReadLatencies).toHaveLength(100);
    expect(rlsReadDomainQueryCounts).toHaveLength(100);
    expect(percentile95(rlsReadLatencies)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1_000);
    expect(percentile95(concurrentRlsReadLatencies)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1_000);
    expectProviderCountersUnchanged(providerCallsBeforeRls);
    console.info(`[CP5-RLS-ACTUAL]${JSON.stringify({ rlsRounds, readLatenciesMs: rlsReadLatencies,
      readP95Ms: percentile95(rlsReadLatencies), readDomainQueriesPerContext: rlsReadDomainQueryCounts,
      concurrentReadLatenciesMs: concurrentRlsReadLatencies, concurrentReadP95Ms: percentile95(concurrentRlsReadLatencies),
      pendingBlockers: Number(pending[0]?.blockers ?? 0n), deadlockBaseline: Number(initialDeadlocks[0]?.deadlocks ?? 0n), deadlockDelta })}`);
    await assertRetainedCohort();
  }, SETUP_TIMEOUT_MS);

  it('accepts and replays the complete signed 1000-message batch within fifteen seconds', async () => {
    await assertRetainedCohort();
    // One full signed 1000-message text batch; no chunk cap, slice, partial success, or cohort reset.
    const batchRows = rowsByBu[0]!.slice(0, 1_000);
    const providerCallsBeforeBatch = providerCounters();
    expect(batchRows.every(({ account }) => account.accountId === accountsByBu[0]![0]!.accountId)).toBe(true);
    const batchMessages = batchRows.map((row, index) => ({ peer: row.peerId,
      id: `synthetic-cp5-1000-batch-${index}-${randomUUID()}`, text: `Text update ${index}: accept the complete signed batch.` }));
    const batchBody = JSON.stringify({ object: 'instagram', entry: [{ id: accountsByBu[0]![0]!.accountId,
      messaging: batchMessages.map(({ peer, id, text }) => ({ sender: { id: peer }, recipient: { id: accountsByBu[0]![0]!.accountId },
        timestamp: Date.now(), message: { mid: id, text } })) }] });
    const batchSignature = signatureFor(batchBody);
    const batchCountBefore = await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId: buIds[0], direction: 'IN', channelOrigin: 'INSTAGRAM' } });
    const batchUnreadBefore = await prisma.conversation.aggregate({ where: { id: { in: batchRows.map(({ conversationId }) => conversationId) } }, _sum: { unreadCount: true } });
    const batchUnreadByIdBefore = new Map((await prisma.conversation.findMany({ where: { id: { in: batchRows.map(({ conversationId }) => conversationId) } },
      select: { id: true, unreadCount: true } })).map(({ id, unreadCount }) => [id, unreadCount]));
    const batchStarted = performance.now();
    const batchResponse = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/instagram').set('content-type', 'application/json')
      .set('x-hub-signature-256', batchSignature).send(batchBody);
    const batchElapsedMs = performance.now() - batchStarted;
    expect(batchResponse.status).toBe(200);
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId: buIds[0], direction: 'IN', channelOrigin: 'INSTAGRAM' } })).toBe(batchCountBefore + 1_000);
    const persistedBatch = await prisma.message.findMany({ where: { externalId: { in: batchMessages.map(({ id }) => id) } },
      select: { externalId: true, buId: true, conversationId: true, providerResourceIdentityId: true, direction: true, channelOrigin: true } });
    expect(persistedBatch).toHaveLength(1_000);
    const batchByExternalId = new Map(persistedBatch.map((message) => [message.externalId, message]));
    const batchRoutes = await prisma.conversationRoute.findMany({ where: { id: { in: batchRows.map(({ routeId }) => routeId) } },
      include: { contactIdentity: true, businessIdentity: true } });
    expect(batchRoutes).toHaveLength(1_000);
    const routeById = new Map(batchRoutes.map((route) => [route.id, route]));
    const batchResources = await prisma.providerResourceIdentity.findMany({
      where: { id: { in: [...new Set(batchRows.map(({ account }) => account.resourceIdentityId))] } },
      select: { id: true, buId: true, provider: true, externalResourceId: true, currentChannelId: true, businessIdentityId: true },
    });
    const resourceById = new Map(batchResources.map((resource) => [resource.id, resource]));
    for (let index = 0; index < batchRows.length; index += 1) {
      const row = batchRows[index]!;
      const route = routeById.get(row.routeId);
      const resource = resourceById.get(row.account.resourceIdentityId);
      expect(batchByExternalId.get(batchMessages[index]!.id)).toMatchObject({ buId: row.buId,
        conversationId: row.conversationId, providerResourceIdentityId: row.account.resourceIdentityId,
        direction: 'IN', channelOrigin: 'INSTAGRAM' });
      expect(route).toMatchObject({ id: row.routeId, buId: row.buId, conversationId: row.conversationId,
        platform: 'INSTAGRAM', contactScopeReference: row.account.scopeReference,
        businessScopeReference: row.account.scopeReference, businessIdentityId: row.account.businessIdentityId,
        contactIdentity: { buId: row.buId, contactId: route?.contactId, platform: 'INSTAGRAM', namespace: 'INSTAGRAM_LOGIN',
          scopeReference: row.account.scopeReference, externalPeerId: row.peerId },
        businessIdentity: { id: row.account.businessIdentityId, buId: row.buId, platform: 'INSTAGRAM',
          namespace: 'INSTAGRAM_LOGIN', scopeReference: row.account.scopeReference, externalResourceId: row.account.accountId } });
      expect(resource).toMatchObject({ id: row.account.resourceIdentityId, buId: row.buId, provider: 'INSTAGRAM',
        externalResourceId: row.account.accountId, currentChannelId: row.account.channelId,
        businessIdentityId: row.account.businessIdentityId });
    }
    expect(batchElapsedMs).toBeLessThanOrEqual(15_000);
    const batchUnreadAfter = await prisma.conversation.aggregate({ where: { id: { in: batchRows.map(({ conversationId }) => conversationId) } }, _sum: { unreadCount: true } });
    expect(batchUnreadAfter._sum.unreadCount).toBe((batchUnreadBefore._sum.unreadCount ?? 0) + 1_000);
    const batchUnreadAfterFirstRows = await prisma.conversation.findMany({ where: { id: { in: batchRows.map(({ conversationId }) => conversationId) } },
      select: { id: true, unreadCount: true } });
    expect(batchUnreadAfterFirstRows).toHaveLength(1_000);
    expect(batchUnreadAfterFirstRows.every(({ id, unreadCount }) => unreadCount === batchUnreadByIdBefore.get(id)! + 1)).toBe(true);
    const exactMessageCountAfterFirst = await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId: buIds[0], direction: 'IN', channelOrigin: 'INSTAGRAM' } });
    const exactUnreadAfterFirst = batchUnreadAfter._sum.unreadCount;
    const replayStarted = performance.now();
    const batchReplay = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/webhooks/instagram').set('content-type', 'application/json')
      .set('x-hub-signature-256', batchSignature).send(batchBody);
    const batchReplayElapsedMs = performance.now() - replayStarted;
    expect(batchReplay.status).toBe(200);
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { buId: buIds[0], direction: 'IN', channelOrigin: 'INSTAGRAM' } })).toBe(exactMessageCountAfterFirst);
    expect((await prisma.conversation.aggregate({ where: { id: { in: batchRows.map(({ conversationId }) => conversationId) } }, _sum: { unreadCount: true } }))._sum.unreadCount)
      .toBe(exactUnreadAfterFirst);
    expect(await prisma.message.count({ where: { externalId: { in: batchMessages.map(({ id }) => id) },
      providerResourceIdentityId: accountsByBu[0]![0]!.resourceIdentityId, direction: 'IN', channelOrigin: 'INSTAGRAM' } })).toBe(1_000);
    const batchUnreadAfterReplay = await prisma.conversation.findMany({ where: { id: { in: batchRows.map(({ conversationId }) => conversationId) } },
      select: { id: true, unreadCount: true } });
    expect(batchUnreadAfterReplay).toHaveLength(1_000);
    const unreadAfterFirstPairs = batchUnreadAfterFirstRows.map(({ id, unreadCount }) => ({ id, unreadCount }))
      .sort((left, right) => left.id.localeCompare(right.id));
    const unreadAfterReplayPairs = batchUnreadAfterReplay.map(({ id, unreadCount }) => ({ id, unreadCount }))
      .sort((left, right) => left.id.localeCompare(right.id));
    expect(unreadAfterReplayPairs).toEqual(unreadAfterFirstPairs);
    expectProviderCountersUnchanged(providerCallsBeforeBatch);
    expect(batchUnreadBefore._sum.unreadCount).toBeLessThan(exactUnreadAfterFirst ?? 0);
    expect(await prisma.conversationRoute.count({ where: { buId: { in: buIds }, platform: 'INSTAGRAM' } })).toBe(20_000);
    console.info(`[CP5-BATCH-ACTUAL]${JSON.stringify({ submitted: batchMessages.length, accepted: 1_000,
      elapsedMs: batchElapsedMs, maximumMs: 15_000, replayElapsedMs: batchReplayElapsedMs,
      replayAdditionalMessages: 0, replayAdditionalUnread: 0, cohortRowsRetained: 20_000,
      batchUnreadBefore: batchUnreadBefore._sum.unreadCount, batchUnreadAfter: exactUnreadAfterFirst })}`);
    await assertRetainedCohort();
  }, SETUP_TIMEOUT_MS);
});


## FULL SOURCE BODY api-app/prisma/schema.prisma

// Schema do núcleo unificado (v1). Nomenclatura conforme ADR-0011:
// estrutura em inglês, Mantenedora e BusinessUnit como nomes próprios do
// domínio, snake_case no banco via @map.
//
// Regra 0 do CLAUDE.md: toda tabela de dado de negócio tem bu_id, e toda
// leitura filtra pelo escopo do usuário. Quem tem mantenedora_id NÃO é dado de
// negócio (app_users, api_keys, mantenedora_settings) e não entra nesse filtro.

generator client {
  provider = "prisma-client-js"
}

datasource db {
  provider = "postgresql"
  url      = env("DATABASE_URL")
}

/* ------------------------------- tenancy -------------------------------- */

enum TenantStatus {
  ATIVO
  INATIVO
}

/// A conta do cliente. Agrupa, endereça e é dona das integrações — não possui
/// conversa e não recebe grant (ADR-0008).
model Mantenedora {
  id        String       @id @default(uuid())
  name      String
  status    TenantStatus @default(ATIVO)
  createdAt DateTime     @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt DateTime     @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  businessUnits BusinessUnit[]
  setores       Setor[]
  arquivos      Arquivo[]
  appUsers      AppUser[]
  apiKeys       ApiKey[]
  settings      MantenedoraSettings?
  typebotBots   TypebotBot[]
  channelRegistrationAttempts ChannelRegistrationAttempt[]
  messageTemplateDrafts MessageTemplateDraft[]
  messageTemplateOperations MessageTemplateOperation[]
  instagramAuthorizationAttempts InstagramAuthorizationAttempt[]

  @@map("mantenedoras")
}

/// A Business Unit é o limite de permissão/atendimento, independente do número.
/// onde conversa e mensagem penduram.
/// Setor — o agrupamento entre a Mantenedora e o número (ADR-0017).
///
/// Ele NÃO é o escopo de permissão: o grant continua materializado na BU, e
/// conceder um setor é EXPANSÃO. É isso que faz nenhuma leitura mudar.
model Setor {
  id            String       @id @default(uuid())
  mantenedoraId String       @map("mantenedora_id")
  mantenedora   Mantenedora  @relation(fields: [mantenedoraId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  name          String
  /// INATIVO some do seletor de cadastro e marca o setor como fora de uso. NÃO
  /// interrompe atendimento: sumir com a inbox de um número por engano de
  /// cadastro seria perder conversa (ADR-0017 §1).
  status        TenantStatus @default(ATIVO)
  createdAt     DateTime     @default(now()) @map("created_at")

  businessUnits BusinessUnit[]
  grants        SetorGrant[]

  @@unique([mantenedoraId, name])
  @@map("setores")
}

/// A CONCESSÃO de um setor a alguém — o fato, separado da consequência.
///
/// As linhas em `user_bu_roles` são a MATERIALIZAÇÃO dela, uma por número. Sem
/// esta tabela, conceder um setor VAZIO não guardaria nada: a concessão só
/// existiria derivada das linhas materializadas, e um setor sem número não teria
/// nenhuma para derivar. O número adicionado depois não encontraria a concessão,
/// e ninguém perceberia — porque o sintoma é um acesso que nunca aparece.
model SetorGrant {
  id        String   @id @default(uuid())
  setorId   String   @map("setor_id")
  setor     Setor    @relation(fields: [setorId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  userId    String   @map("user_id")
  user      AppUser  @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  role      Role
  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at")

  @@unique([setorId, userId])
  @@index([userId])
  @@map("setor_grants")
}

model BusinessUnit {
  id            String       @id @default(uuid())
  mantenedoraId String       @map("mantenedora_id")
  mantenedora   Mantenedora  @relation(fields: [mantenedoraId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  /// OBRIGATÓRIO (ADR-0017 §1): número sem setor é número que nenhum atendente
  /// enxerga. Toda Mantenedora nasce com o setor `Geral`, e é nele que cai quem
  /// não escolher.
  setorId       String       @map("setor_id")
  setor         Setor        @relation(fields: [setorId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  name          String
  /// Telefone em E.164, só dígitos. Nulo enquanto o canal não informar.
  phone         String?
  status        TenantStatus @default(ATIVO)
  /// Rastreio da origem na migração (ADR-0003). Sai quando ninguém mais cruzar
  /// com o sistema antigo.
  legacyRef     String?      @map("legacy_ref")
  createdAt     DateTime     @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt     DateTime     @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  channels      BuChannel[]
  roles         UserBuRole[]
  conversations Conversation[]
  channelRegistrationAttempts ChannelRegistrationAttempt[]
  providerResourceIdentities ProviderResourceIdentity[]
  contacts Contact[]
  contactIdentities ContactIdentity[]
  businessChannelIdentities BusinessChannelIdentity[]
  messageTemplateDrafts MessageTemplateDraft[]
  messageTemplateOperations MessageTemplateOperation[]
  messageTemplateObservationLeases MessageTemplateObservationLease[]
  instagramAuthorizationAttempts InstagramAuthorizationAttempt[]

  @@unique([id, mantenedoraId])
  @@index([mantenedoraId, status])
  @@index([setorId])
  @@map("business_units")
}

enum ChannelType {
  OFICIAL
  NAO_OFICIAL
  INSTAGRAM
}

enum ChannelPlatform {
  WHATSAPP
  INSTAGRAM
}

enum ChannelStatus {
  CONECTANDO
  CONECTADO
  DESCONECTADO
  DESLOGADO
}

/// A binding to one transport adapter for an owned business resource.
model BuChannel {
  id     String       @id @default(uuid())
  buId   String       @map("bu_id")
  bu     BusinessUnit @relation(fields: [buId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  type   ChannelType
  status ChannelStatus @default(CONECTANDO)
  generation Int @default(1)
  currentAttemptId String? @unique @map("current_attempt_id")
  currentAttempt ChannelRegistrationAttempt? @relation("CurrentChannelAttempt", fields: [currentAttemptId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)
  pairingEpoch Int @default(0) @map("pairing_epoch")
  ownNumberVerified Boolean @default(false) @map("own_number_verified")
  verifiedEpoch Int? @map("verified_epoch")
  providerConnectionState String @default("PENDING") @map("provider_connection_state")
  receiptVerifiedAt DateTime? @map("receipt_verified_at")
  operationLeaseToken String? @map("operation_lease_token")
  operationLeaseUntil DateTime? @map("operation_lease_until")
  pendingCleanupInstanceName String? @map("pending_cleanup_instance_name")
  pendingCleanupIdentityId String? @map("pending_cleanup_identity_id")
  businessIdentityId String? @map("business_identity_id")
  businessIdentity BusinessChannelIdentity? @relation(fields: [businessIdentityId, buId], references: [id, buId], onDelete: Restrict)

  /// Canal OFICIAL (Meta Cloud API)
  wabaId        String? @map("waba_id")
  phoneNumberId String? @map("phone_number_id")

  /// Canal NAO_OFICIAL (Evolution API)
  evolutionInstanceName String? @unique @map("evolution_instance_name")

  /// A janela de 24h da Meta é atributo do CANAL, não do produto (ADR-0009).
  requiresWindow Boolean   @default(false) @map("requires_window")
  connectedAt    DateTime? @map("connected_at")
  createdAt      DateTime  @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt      DateTime  @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  credential ChannelCredential?
  attempts ChannelRegistrationAttempt[]
  providerIdentities ProviderResourceIdentity[]
  instagramAuthorizationAttempts InstagramAuthorizationAttempt[]
  @@unique([id, buId, businessIdentityId], map: "bu_channels_id_bu_id_business_identity_id_key")
  @@unique([id, buId, businessIdentityId, type], map: "bu_channels_current_provider_owner_key")
  // Adapter and ownership cardinality uses SQL-only partial unique indexes.
  @@map("bu_channels")
}

enum ChannelRegistrationState {
  PENDING_AUTH
  PENDING_NUMBER
  REGISTERING
  PENDING_RECEIPT
  CONNECTED
  ERROR
  CANCELLED
}

enum ChannelRegistrationOperation {
  NOVO
  REAUTORIZAR
}

/// Registration workflow state is deliberately separate from ChannelStatus,
/// which preserves the existing transport-facing status contract.
model ChannelRegistrationAttempt {
  id String @id @default(uuid())
  mantenedoraId String @map("mantenedora_id")
  mantenedora Mantenedora @relation(fields: [mantenedoraId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  buId String @map("bu_id")
  bu BusinessUnit @relation(fields: [buId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  channelId String? @map("channel_id")
  channel BuChannel? @relation(fields: [channelId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  currentForChannel BuChannel? @relation("CurrentChannelAttempt")
  type ChannelType
  operation ChannelRegistrationOperation @default(NOVO)
  userId String @map("user_id")
  sessionHash String @map("session_hash")
  nonceHash String? @map("nonce_hash")
  idempotencyKey String @map("idempotency_key")
  requestHash String @map("request_hash")
  state ChannelRegistrationState @default(PENDING_AUTH)
  version Int @default(1)
  generation Int @default(1)
  pairingEpoch Int? @map("pairing_epoch")
  leaseUntil DateTime? @map("lease_until")
  expiresAt DateTime @map("expires_at")
  stage String @default("RESERVED")
  safeErrorCode String? @map("safe_error_code")
  candidateKeyId String? @map("candidate_key_id")
  candidateNonce Bytes? @map("candidate_nonce")
  candidateCiphertext Bytes? @map("candidate_ciphertext")
  candidateTag Bytes? @map("candidate_tag")
  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  @@unique([mantenedoraId, idempotencyKey])
  @@index([buId, type, state])
  @@index([state, expiresAt])
  @@map("channel_registration_attempts")
}

/// Active encrypted credential. Ciphertext is only ever passed to adapters
/// through the channel credential resolver; never project this model publicly.
model ChannelCredential {
  id String @id @default(uuid())
  channelId String @unique @map("channel_id")
  channel BuChannel @relation(fields: [channelId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  provider ChannelType
  buId String @map("bu_id")
  generation Int
  version Int @default(1)
  acquiredAt DateTime? @map("acquired_at")
  expiresAt DateTime? @map("expires_at")
  keyId String @map("key_id")
  nonce Bytes
  ciphertext Bytes
  tag Bytes
  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  @@index([buId, provider])
  @@map("channel_credentials")
}

/// Durable Instagram Login reservation and candidate. OAuth codes and plaintext
/// state are deliberately absent; candidate credentials remain encrypted until
/// the operator explicitly confirms the verified professional account.
model InstagramAuthorizationAttempt {
  id String @id @default(uuid())
  mantenedoraId String @map("mantenedora_id")
  mantenedora Mantenedora @relation(fields: [mantenedoraId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  buId String @map("bu_id")
  bu BusinessUnit @relation(fields: [buId, mantenedoraId], references: [id, mantenedoraId], onDelete: Restrict)
  userId String @map("user_id")
  user AppUser @relation(fields: [userId, mantenedoraId], references: [id, mantenedoraId], onDelete: Restrict)
  sessionHash String @map("session_hash")
  idempotencyKey String @map("idempotency_key")
  requestHash String @map("request_hash")
  operation String
  channelId String? @map("channel_id")
  channel BuChannel? @relation(fields: [channelId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  expectedGeneration Int? @map("expected_generation")
  expectedCredentialVersion Int? @map("expected_credential_version")
  mappedCredentialVersion Int? @map("mapped_credential_version")
  stateHash String @map("state_hash")
  status String @default("AUTHORIZATION_PENDING")
  expiresAt DateTime @map("expires_at")
  callbackLeaseToken String? @map("callback_lease_token")
  callbackLeaseUntil DateTime? @map("callback_lease_until")
  authorizationReference String? @map("authorization_reference")
  professionalUserId String? @map("professional_user_id")
  username String?
  candidateKeyId String? @map("candidate_key_id")
  candidateNonce Bytes? @map("candidate_nonce")
  candidateCiphertext Bytes? @map("candidate_ciphertext")
  candidateTag Bytes? @map("candidate_tag")
  acquiredAt DateTime? @map("acquired_at")
  credentialExpiresAt DateTime? @map("credential_expires_at")
  subscriptionState String @default("NOT_STARTED") @map("subscription_state")
  subscriptionStartedAt DateTime? @map("subscription_started_at")
  lastSubscriptionAttemptAt DateTime? @map("last_subscription_attempt_at")
  subscriptionRequests Int @default(0) @map("subscription_requests")
  confirmationIdempotencyKey String? @map("confirmation_idempotency_key")
  confirmationRequestHash String? @map("confirmation_request_hash")
  confirmationState String? @map("confirmation_state")
  confirmationResultStatus String? @map("confirmation_result_status")
  confirmationResultChannelId String? @map("confirmation_result_channel_id")
  confirmationResultSubscriptionState String? @map("confirmation_result_subscription_state")
  operationLeaseToken String? @map("operation_lease_token")
  operationLeaseUntil DateTime? @map("operation_lease_until")
  operationDeadlineAt DateTime? @map("operation_deadline_at")
  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  @@unique([mantenedoraId, idempotencyKey])
  @@index([userId, createdAt])
  @@index([buId, createdAt])
  @@index([status, expiresAt])
  @@index([professionalUserId, subscriptionRequests], map: "ig_auth_attempt_prof_user_subreq_idx")
  @@index([professionalUserId, subscriptionState], map: "ig_auth_attempt_prof_user_substate_idx")
  @@map("instagram_authorization_attempts")
}

/// Durable ownership tombstone: deleting a channel detaches currentChannelId,
/// while buId remains RESTRICT-owned and cannot be transferred. A PostgreSQL
/// trigger also makes (provider, externalResourceId, buId) immutable after create.
model ProviderResourceIdentity {
  id String @id @default(uuid())
  provider ChannelType
  externalResourceId String @map("external_resource_id")
  buId String @map("bu_id")
  bu BusinessUnit @relation(fields: [buId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  currentChannelId String? @map("current_channel_id")
  currentChannel BuChannel? @relation(fields: [currentChannelId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)
  businessIdentityId String? @map("business_identity_id")
  businessIdentity BusinessChannelIdentity? @relation(fields: [businessIdentityId, buId], references: [id, buId], onDelete: Restrict)
  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  retiredAt DateTime? @map("retired_at")
  managedLifecycle Boolean @default(false) @map("managed_lifecycle")
  unverifiedStaging Boolean @default(false) @map("unverified_staging")
  cleanupState String @default("UNMANAGED") @map("cleanup_state")
  cleanupLeaseToken String? @map("cleanup_lease_token")
  cleanupLeaseUntil DateTime? @map("cleanup_lease_until")
  instagramOperationLeaseToken String? @map("instagram_operation_lease_token")
  instagramOperationLeaseUntil DateTime? @map("instagram_operation_lease_until")
  instagramLastSubscriptionAttemptAt DateTime? @map("instagram_last_subscription_attempt_at")
  instagramSubscriptionPostDispatchStartedAt DateTime? @map("instagram_subscription_post_dispatch_started_at")
  messages Message[]
  messageTemplateDrafts MessageTemplateDraft[]
  @@unique([id, buId])
  @@unique([id, buId, businessIdentityId, currentChannelId, provider], map: "provider_resource_identities_route_owner_key")
  @@unique([provider, externalResourceId])
  @@index([buId])
  @@index([currentChannelId])
  @@map("provider_resource_identities")
}

/// Durable business resource ownership, independent from transport/provider IDs.
model BusinessChannelIdentity {
  id String @id @default(uuid())
  buId String @map("bu_id")
  bu BusinessUnit @relation(fields: [buId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  platform ChannelPlatform
  namespace String
  /// WhatsApp business ownership uses the BU scope; Instagram uses its verified account/app scope.
  scopeReference String @map("scope_reference")
  externalResourceId String @map("external_resource_id")
  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at")

  channels BuChannel[]
  providerResourceIdentities ProviderResourceIdentity[]
  conversationRoutes ConversationRoute[]

  @@unique([id, buId])
  @@unique([id, buId, platform, scopeReference], map: "business_channel_identities_id_bu_platform_scope_key")
  @@unique([platform, namespace, externalResourceId], map: "business_channel_identities_platform_namespace_resource_key")
  @@index([buId, platform])
  @@map("business_channel_identities")
}

/* ------------------------------ identidade ------------------------------ */

/// De onde veio o grant (ADR-0017 §2). DIRETO é concessão nominal; os outros
/// dois são expansão, e carregam o id da origem em `granted_via_id`.
enum OrigemDoGrant {
  DIRETO
  SETOR
  MANTENEDORA
}

/// Três papéis (ADR-0018). VIEWER saiu, e o eixo de capacidades também:
/// disponibilidade passou a ser MÓDULO (ADR-0016), não permissão de usuário.
enum Role {
  OWNER
  ADMIN
  ATENDENTE
}

/// Pessoa que usa o produto. Sempre de UMA Mantenedora; e-mail único dentro
/// dela, não globalmente (ADR-0008 §3).
model AppUser {
  id            String       @id @default(uuid())
  mantenedoraId String       @map("mantenedora_id")
  mantenedora   Mantenedora  @relation(fields: [mantenedoraId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  name          String
  email         String
  /// bcrypt (ADR-0007). Hash PBKDF2 importado é reconhecido pelo prefixo e
  /// reidratado no primeiro login.
  /// NULO enquanto o convite não foi usado (ADR-0025 §2): pessoa sem credencial
  /// existe no cadastro e não entra.
  passwordHash  String?      @map("password_hash")
  /// Só para o hash PBKDF2 legado, que guardava o salt em coluna separada.
  /// Limpo na reidratação.
  legacySalt    String?      @map("legacy_salt")
  /// Contato da pessoa, editável por ela mesma no perfil. Não é credencial e
  /// não participa de login — o e-mail é que identifica (ADR-0007).
  phone         String?
  /// Id do arquivo no armazenamento (ADR-0024), nunca um caminho.
  avatar        String?
  status        TenantStatus @default(ATIVO)
  mustChangePassword Boolean  @default(false) @map("must_change_password")
  passwordChangedAt  DateTime? @map("password_changed_at")
  createdAt          DateTime  @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt          DateTime  @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  roles       UserBuRole[]
  createdMessageTemplateDrafts MessageTemplateDraft[]
  messageTemplateOperations MessageTemplateOperation[]
  instagramAuthorizationAttempts InstagramAuthorizationAttempt[]
  setorGrants SetorGrant[]
  tokens      TokenDeAcesso[]

  @@unique([id, mantenedoraId])
  @@unique([mantenedoraId, email])
  @@map("app_users")
}

/// A única fonte de permissão. bu_id é SEMPRE uma BU — não existe grant em
/// Mantenedora; o papel nela é calculado (ADR-0008 §4).
model UserBuRole {
  id     String       @id @default(uuid())
  userId String       @map("user_id")
  user   AppUser      @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  buId   String       @map("bu_id")
  bu     BusinessUnit @relation(fields: [buId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  role   Role
  /// Proveniência, em PAR (ADR-0017 §2). Era um campo só, que só distinguia
  /// direto de expansão da Mantenedora; com o Setor há três origens, e o tipo
  /// precisa ser explícito. Grant DIRETO vence expansão, e revogar uma origem
  /// remove só o que ela criou.
  grantedViaTipo OrigemDoGrant @default(DIRETO) @map("granted_via_tipo")
  grantedViaId   String?       @map("granted_via_id")
  createdAt      DateTime      @default(now()) @map("created_at")

  @@unique([userId, buId])
  @@index([buId])
  @@map("user_bu_roles")
}

/// Tentativas de senha erradas, por e-mail (ADR-0007 §4).
model LoginAttempt {
  email        String    @id
  fails        Int       @default(0)
  lastFailAt   DateTime? @map("last_fail_at")
  blockedUntil DateTime? @map("blocked_until")

  @@map("login_attempts")
}

/* ----------------------------- atendimento ------------------------------ */

enum ConversationStatus {
  QUEUED
  IN_SERVICE
  CLOSED
}

enum ContactType {
  PESSOA
  GRUPO
}

enum AttendantOrigin {
  INTERNO
  EXTERNO
}

/// Conversa é entidade e pendura na BU (ADR-0009). Chave natural (bu_id,
/// contact); o JID fica no adaptador, nunca no domínio.
model Conversation {
  id      String       @id @default(uuid())
  buId    String       @map("bu_id")
  bu      BusinessUnit @relation(fields: [buId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  /// Compatibility projection retained for the current inbox contract; qualified identity is authoritative.
  contact     String
  contactId String @map("contact_id")
  localContact Contact @relation(fields: [contactId, buId], references: [id, buId], onDelete: Restrict)
  contactType ContactType        @default(PESSOA) @map("contact_type")
  displayName String?            @map("display_name")
  status      ConversationStatus @default(QUEUED)

  /// Atribuição: um conceito com proveniência (ADR-0009 §2). FK opcional com
  /// SetNull + snapshot de nome (ADR-0007).
  attendantUserId String?          @map("attendant_user_id")
  attendantName   String?          @map("attendant_name")
  attendantOrigin AttendantOrigin? @map("attendant_origin")

  unreadCount   Int       @default(0) @map("unread_count")
  lastMessageAt DateTime? @map("last_message_at")
  createdAt     DateTime  @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt     DateTime  @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  messages Message[]
  routes ConversationRoute[]
  dispatchAdmissions DispatchAdmission[]
  initialMessageIntents InitialMessageIntent[]

  @@unique([buId, contactId])
  @@unique([id, buId, contactId])
  @@index([buId, status, lastMessageAt])
  @@index([buId, contact])
  @@map("conversations")
}

/// BU-local person or group record, intentionally independent of external IDs.
model Contact {
  id String @id @default(uuid())
  buId String @map("bu_id")
  bu BusinessUnit @relation(fields: [buId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  displayName String? @map("display_name")
  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at")

  identities ContactIdentity[]
  conversations Conversation[]
  routes ConversationRoute[]

  @@unique([id, buId])
  @@map("contacts")
}

/// Qualified external peer identity; username/display labels are not keys.
model ContactIdentity {
  id String @id @default(uuid())
  buId String @map("bu_id")
  contactId String @map("contact_id")
  bu BusinessUnit @relation(fields: [buId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  contact Contact @relation(fields: [contactId, buId], references: [id, buId], onDelete: Cascade)
  platform ChannelPlatform
  namespace String
  scopeReference String @map("scope_reference")
  externalPeerId String @map("external_peer_id")
  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at")

  conversationRoutes ConversationRoute[]

  @@unique([id, buId, contactId, platform, scopeReference], map: "contact_identities_id_bu_contact_platform_scope_key")
  @@unique([buId, platform, namespace, scopeReference, externalPeerId], map: "contact_identities_bu_platform_namespace_scope_peer_key")
  @@index([contactId, buId])
  @@map("contact_identities")
}

/// Immutable, locally proven address from one conversation to one owned resource.
model ConversationRoute {
  id String @id @default(uuid())
  buId String @map("bu_id")
  conversationId String @map("conversation_id")
  contactId String @map("contact_id")
  contactIdentityId String @map("contact_identity_id")
  platform ChannelPlatform
  contactScopeReference String @map("contact_scope_reference")
  businessScopeReference String @map("business_scope_reference")
  businessIdentityId String @map("business_identity_id")
  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at")

  conversation Conversation @relation(fields: [conversationId, buId, contactId], references: [id, buId, contactId], onDelete: Restrict)
  contact Contact @relation(fields: [contactId, buId], references: [id, buId], onDelete: Restrict)
  contactIdentity ContactIdentity @relation(fields: [contactIdentityId, buId, contactId, platform, contactScopeReference], references: [id, buId, contactId, platform, scopeReference], onDelete: Restrict)
  businessIdentity BusinessChannelIdentity @relation(fields: [businessIdentityId, buId, platform, businessScopeReference], references: [id, buId, platform, scopeReference], onDelete: Restrict)
  @@unique([conversationId, contactIdentityId, businessIdentityId])
  @@index([conversationId, createdAt, id])
  @@map("conversation_routes")
}

enum DispatchAdmissionCaller {
  BFF
  PUBLIC
  BOT
}

enum DispatchAdmissionState {
  PREPARED
  SENDING
  SETTLED
  UNCERTAIN
}

enum InitialMessageIntentState {
  PREPARED
  SENDING
  EXTERNAL_ACCEPTED
  EXTERNAL_ACCEPTED_LOCAL_BLOCKED
  SUCCEEDED
  REJECTED
  AMBIGUOUS
}

/// Finite exclusion token for one ordinary outbound attempt; it is not a Message or retry owner.
model DispatchAdmission {
  id              String                  @id @default(uuid())
  conversationId  String                  @map("conversation_id")
  conversation    Conversation            @relation(fields: [conversationId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  buId            String                  @map("bu_id")
  caller           DispatchAdmissionCaller
  actorId          String                  @map("actor_id")
  ownerNonce       String                  @map("owner_nonce")
  selectedContext  Json                    @map("selected_context")
  state            DispatchAdmissionState  @default(PREPARED)
  expiresAt        DateTime                @map("expires_at") @db.Timestamp(3)
  dispatchDeadlineAt DateTime?             @map("dispatch_deadline_at") @db.Timestamp(3)
  releasedAt       DateTime?               @map("released_at") @db.Timestamp(3)
  riskAcknowledgedAt DateTime?             @map("risk_acknowledged_at") @db.Timestamp(3)
  riskAcknowledgedByActorId String?        @map("risk_acknowledged_by_actor_id")
  createdAt        DateTime                @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamp(3)
  updatedAt        DateTime                @updatedAt @map("updated_at") @db.Timestamp(3)

  @@unique([ownerNonce])
  @@index([conversationId, releasedAt, state, expiresAt], map: "dispatch_admissions_active_conversation_idx")
  // Intentional SQL-only partial ordered index: PostgreSQL can use the releasedAt IS NULL predicate for both bounded active lookups and conversation-wide conflict checks; Prisma cannot represent this partial predicate.
  @@map("dispatch_admissions")
}

/// Durable first-send request and exclusive conversation slot; Message remains owned by MessageAcceptanceService.
model InitialMessageIntent {
  id                    String                     @id @default(uuid())
  conversationId        String                     @map("conversation_id")
  conversation          Conversation               @relation(fields: [conversationId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  buId                  String                     @map("bu_id")
  actorId               String                     @map("actor_id")
  idempotencyKey        String                     @map("idempotency_key")
  ownerNonce            String                     @map("owner_nonce")
  requestFingerprint    String                     @map("request_fingerprint")
  request               Json
  selectedContext       Json                       @map("selected_context")
  state                 InitialMessageIntentState @default(PREPARED)
  leaseExpiresAt        DateTime                   @map("lease_expires_at") @db.Timestamp(3)
  dispatchDeadlineAt    DateTime?                  @map("dispatch_deadline_at") @db.Timestamp(3)
  externalMessageId     String?                    @map("external_message_id")
  slotReleasedAt        DateTime?                  @map("slot_released_at") @db.Timestamp(3)
  riskAcknowledgedAt    DateTime?                  @map("risk_acknowledged_at") @db.Timestamp(3)
  riskAcknowledgedByActorId String?                @map("risk_acknowledged_by_actor_id")
  createdAt             DateTime                   @default(now()) @map("created_at") @db.Timestamp(3)
  updatedAt             DateTime                   @updatedAt @map("updated_at") @db.Timestamp(3)

  @@unique([buId, actorId, idempotencyKey], map: "initial_message_intents_idempotency_key")
  @@unique([ownerNonce])
  @@index([conversationId, slotReleasedAt, state, createdAt], map: "initial_message_intents_slot_idx")
  @@index([conversationId, buId, createdAt(sort: Desc), id(sort: Desc)], map: "initial_message_intents_options_order_idx")
  @@index([actorId, createdAt(sort: Desc)], map: "initial_message_intents_actor_rate_idx")
  @@map("initial_message_intents")
}

enum MessageDirection {
  IN
  OUT
}

enum MessageType {
  TEXTO
  IMAGEM
  AUDIO
  VIDEO
  DOCUMENTO
  OUTRO
}

enum MessageStatus {
  RECEBIDA
  ENVIADA
  ENTREGUE
  LIDA
  FALHOU
}

model Message {
  id             String       @id @default(uuid())
  conversationId String       @map("conversation_id")
  conversation   Conversation @relation(fields: [conversationId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  /// Denormalizado do conversation para que TODA tabela de dado de negócio
  /// tenha bu_id (regra 0).
  buId          String           @map("bu_id")
  direction     MessageDirection
  messageType   MessageType      @default(TEXTO) @map("message_type")
  content       String?
  mediaRef      String?          @map("media_ref")
  /// Por qual canal entrou ou saiu (ADR-0009 §1).
  channelOrigin ChannelType      @map("channel_origin")
  status        MessageStatus
  externalId    String?          @map("external_id")
  providerResourceIdentityId String? @map("provider_resource_identity_id")
  providerResourceIdentity ProviderResourceIdentity? @relation(fields: [providerResourceIdentityId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict)
  /// Autoria: FK opcional SetNull + snapshot (ADR-0007). O payload cru NÃO
  /// mora aqui — só no log de webhook, com expurgo (ADR-0009 §7).
  authorUserId String?  @map("author_user_id")
  authorName   String?  @map("author_name")
  /// Only a signed, verified Meta IN timestamp; never a timeline timestamp.
  officialInboundAt DateTime? @map("official_inbound_at")
  createdAt    DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at")

  @@index([conversationId, createdAt])
  @@index([conversationId, direction, createdAt, id], map: "messages_conversation_direction_created_id_idx")
  @@index([conversationId, providerResourceIdentityId, direction, channelOrigin, officialInboundAt, id], map: "messages_conversation_trusted_window_idx")
  @@index([buId, createdAt])
  @@unique([providerResourceIdentityId, externalId])
  @@map("messages")
}

/* ------------------------------ integração ------------------------------ */

/// Chave da API pública. Pertence a uma Mantenedora; escopo nulo = todas as
/// BUs DELA, nunca do sistema (ADR-0008 §6, ADR-0010).
model ApiKey {
  id            String      @id @default(uuid())
  mantenedoraId String      @map("mantenedora_id")
  mantenedora   Mantenedora @relation(fields: [mantenedoraId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  name          String
  /// Só o SHA-256. A chave em claro aparece uma vez, na criação.
  keyHash    String    @unique @map("key_hash")
  keyPrefix  String    @map("key_prefix")
  scopeBuIds String[]  @map("scope_bu_ids")
  lastUsedAt DateTime? @map("last_used_at")
  revokedAt  DateTime? @map("revoked_at")
  createdByName String? @map("created_by_name")
  createdAt  DateTime  @default(now()) @map("created_at")

  @@index([mantenedoraId])
  @@map("api_keys")
}

/// Webhook de saída, agora POR MANTENEDORA — deixou de ser singleton
/// (ADR-0008 §6).
model MantenedoraSettings {
  mantenedoraId         String      @id @map("mantenedora_id")
  mantenedora           Mantenedora @relation(fields: [mantenedoraId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  outboundWebhookUrl    String?     @map("outbound_webhook_url")
  outboundWebhookSecret String?     @map("outbound_webhook_secret")

  /// O que ESTA Mantenedora contratou (ADR-0016). O teto continua sendo a env:
  /// o efetivo é a interseção dos dois, e é ela que o /me devolve.
  /// Chave ausente = módulo desligado.
  modulos               Json        @default("{}")

  /// Marca, escolhida no wizard. Vira token de CSS no front, nunca env de build.
  marcaNome             String?     @map("marca_nome")
  marcaCor              String?     @map("marca_cor")
  marcaCorClaro         String?     @map("marca_cor_claro")
  marcaCorEscuro        String?     @map("marca_cor_escuro")
  marcaCorSecundaria    String?     @map("marca_cor_secundaria")
  /// Id do arquivo no armazenamento (ADR-0024), nunca um caminho nem uma URL.
  marcaLogo             String?     @map("marca_logo")
  marcaTema             String      @default("claro") @map("marca_tema")

  /// Subdomínio do cliente. Na v1 é RÓTULO, não roteamento (ADR-0016 §5): a
  /// Mantenedora continua vindo da sessão.
  host                  String?
  /// Remetente dos e-mails de convite.
  remetente             String?
  /// Papel padrão de quem entra por convite. OWNER é recusado — seria
  /// transferência de titularidade disfarçada de cadastro (ADR-0018 §4).
  papelConvite          Role        @default(ATENDENTE) @map("papel_convite")

  /// Enquanto false, todo o resto do BFF responde 409 ONBOARDING_PENDENTE
  /// (ADR-0016 §4). Painel sem módulo escolhido não tem o que mostrar.
  onboardingConcluido   Boolean     @default(false) @map("onboarding_concluido")

  updatedAt             DateTime    @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  @@map("mantenedora_settings")
}

enum TypebotBotStatus {
  ATIVO
  INATIVO
}

enum TypebotTriggerKind {
  PALAVRA_CHAVE
  NOVA_CONVERSA
}

/// Catálogo local dos bots Typebot Cloud. O fluxo continua no Typebot; escopo,
/// ativação e vínculo com a conta continuam no LeadsHug.
model TypebotBot {
  id            String          @id @default(uuid())
  mantenedoraId String          @map("mantenedora_id")
  mantenedora   Mantenedora     @relation(fields: [mantenedoraId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  typebotId     String          @map("typebot_id")
  publishedTypebotId String?    @map("published_typebot_id")
  name          String
  status        TypebotBotStatus @default(ATIVO)
  editorUrl     String          @map("editor_url")
  createdAt     DateTime        @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt     DateTime        @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  triggers TypebotTrigger[]
  sessions TypebotSession[]

  @@unique([mantenedoraId, typebotId])
  @@index([mantenedoraId, status])
  @@map("typebot_bots")
}

model TypebotTrigger {
  id        String             @id @default(uuid())
  botId     String             @map("bot_id")
  bot       TypebotBot         @relation(fields: [botId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  kind      TypebotTriggerKind
  value     String
  buId      String?            @map("bu_id")
  active    Boolean            @default(true)
  createdAt DateTime           @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt DateTime           @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  @@index([botId, active])
  @@map("typebot_triggers")
}

model TypebotSession {
  id            String     @id @default(uuid())
  botId         String     @map("bot_id")
  bot           TypebotBot @relation(fields: [botId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  mantenedoraId String     @map("mantenedora_id")
  buId          String     @map("bu_id")
  contato       String
  sessionId     String     @map("session_id")
  ativo         Boolean    @default(true)
  createdAt     DateTime   @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt     DateTime   @updatedAt @map("updated_at")

  @@unique([botId, buId, contato])
  @@index([mantenedoraId, buId, contato])
  @@map("typebot_sessions")
}

/// Quem é dono de cada arquivo (ADR-0024 §5). O binário mora no volume; esta
/// tabela é o que permite barrar a leitura por escopo.
model Arquivo {
  id            String      @id
  mantenedoraId String      @map("mantenedora_id")
  mantenedora   Mantenedora @relation(fields: [mantenedoraId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  tipo          String
  bytes         Int
  createdAt     DateTime    @default(now()) @map("created_at")

  @@index([mantenedoraId])
  @@map("arquivos")
}

/// Convite e recuperação de senha — o mesmo mecanismo, validade diferente
/// (ADR-0025 §3).
enum TipoDeToken {
  CONVITE
  RECUPERACAO
}

/// O token vive HASHEADO: o valor em claro existe uma vez, no link. Vazamento do
/// banco não vira vazamento de contas — mesmo raciocínio da chave de API.
model TokenDeAcesso {
  id        String      @id @default(uuid())
  userId    String      @map("user_id")
  user      AppUser     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  tipo      TipoDeToken
  /// sha256 do valor. Único, para que a busca seja por igualdade e não varredura.
  hash      String      @unique
  expiraEm  DateTime    @map("expira_em")
  /// Uso ÚNICO: preenchido na mesma transação em que a senha é gravada.
  usadoEm   DateTime?   @map("usado_em")
  createdAt DateTime    @default(now()) @map("created_at")

  @@index([userId, tipo])
  @@map("tokens_de_acesso")
}

/* -------------------------- auditoria e webhook -------------------------- */

/// Ação humana auditável, transversal aos dois canais (ADR-0007 §5).
/// Autoria por snapshot: sobrevive à exclusão do usuário.
/// O escopo da linha (ADR-0020 §1.1). Existe para tornar o vazamento
/// IRREPRESENTÁVEL, não improvável: com `bu_id` nulável, NULL significaria "ação
/// da conta" E "alguém esqueceu de preencher" ao mesmo tempo, e a leitura teria
/// de virar `bu_id IS NULL OR ...`, que é a forma que a regra 0 proíbe.
enum EscopoDaAtividade {
  BU
  CONTA
}

/// As seis categorias do filtro (ADR-0020 §1). Cinco vêm do desenho; EXPORTACAO
/// é a sexta, e a divergência é deliberada.
enum CategoriaDeAtividade {
  CRIAR
  EDITAR
  MOVER
  ATENDIMENTO
  TAG
  EXPORTACAO
}

enum EntidadeDaAtividade {
  CARD
  CONVERSA
  NUMERO
  SETOR
  USUARIO
  INTEGRACAO
  CONFIGURACAO
}

/// O rastro das ações dos usuários (ADR-0020). NÃO é módulo: é núcleo, grava
/// sempre, e **não expurga** — o volume é texto curto, e auditoria com data de
/// validade falha justamente na pergunta antiga.
model ActivityLog {
  id            String   @id @default(uuid())
  mantenedoraId String   @map("mantenedora_id")

  escopo        EscopoDaAtividade
  /// Obrigatório quando escopo=BU, PROIBIDO quando escopo=CONTA. O CHECK no
  /// banco é quem cobra — não este comentário.
  buId          String?  @map("bu_id")

  userId        String?  @map("user_id")
  /// CONGELADO: o nome de quem agiu, naquele dia. Sobrevive à remoção da pessoa
  /// e ao renome de qualquer coisa.
  userName      String?  @map("user_name")
  userEmail     String?  @map("user_email")

  categoria     CategoriaDeAtividade
  entidade      EntidadeDaAtividade
  entidadeId    String?  @map("entidade_id")
  campo         String?
  de            String?
  para          String?
  /// Só para categoria que declare um formato no ADR-0022 — hoje, EXPORTACAO.
  detalhe       Json?

  /// A frase pronta, em português, CONGELADA na escrita.
  texto         String

  /// Compatibilidade com o que a v1 já gravava; some quando ninguém mais ler.
  action        String
  detail        String?
  ip            String?
  createdAt     DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at")

  @@index([mantenedoraId, createdAt])
  @@index([entidade, entidadeId])
  @@map("activity_log")
}

/// O payload cru vive AQUI e só aqui, com expurgo de 90 dias (ADR-0009 §7).
model WebhookEvent {
  id        String      @id @default(uuid())
  channel   ChannelType
  buId      String?     @map("bu_id")
  eventType String      @map("event_type")
  payload   Json
  receivedAt DateTime   @default(now()) @map("received_at")

  @@index([receivedAt])
  @@map("webhook_events")
}

enum MessageTemplateDraftState {
  DRAFT
  LOCKED
}

/// Safe local draft snapshot. Provider identities and account references stay server-side.
model MessageTemplateDraft {
  id String @id @default(uuid())
  mantenedoraId String @map("mantenedora_id")
  mantenedora Mantenedora @relation(fields: [mantenedoraId], references: [id], onDelete: Restrict, map: "mt_draft_mantenedora_fkey")
  buId String @map("bu_id")
  bu BusinessUnit @relation(fields: [buId, mantenedoraId], references: [id, mantenedoraId], onDelete: Restrict, map: "mt_draft_bu_fkey")
  creatorUserId String @map("creator_user_id")
  creator AppUser @relation(fields: [creatorUserId, mantenedoraId], references: [id, mantenedoraId], onDelete: Restrict, map: "mt_draft_creator_fkey")
  /// Historical immutable BuChannel id; the channel row is transient and may be detached.
  channelId String @map("channel_id")
  providerResourceIdentityId String @map("provider_resource_identity_id")
  providerResourceIdentity ProviderResourceIdentity @relation(fields: [providerResourceIdentityId, buId], references: [id, buId], onDelete: Restrict, map: "mt_draft_identity_fkey")
  accountReference String @map("account_reference") @db.VarChar(128)
  channelGeneration Int @map("channel_generation")
  pairingEpoch Int @map("pairing_epoch")
  activeCredentialVersion Int? @map("active_credential_version")
  clientRequestId String @map("client_request_id") @db.VarChar(128)
  requestHash String @map("request_hash") @db.Char(64)
  state MessageTemplateDraftState @default(DRAFT)
  revision Int @default(1)
  definition Json
  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at")
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt @map("updated_at")
  revisions MessageTemplateRevision[]
  operations MessageTemplateOperation[]

  @@unique([id, buId, mantenedoraId], map: "mt_draft_id_bu_tenant_uq")

  @@unique([buId, creatorUserId, clientRequestId], map: "mt_draft_bu_creator_req_uq")
  @@index([buId, creatorUserId, createdAt, id], map: "mt_draft_bu_creator_created_idx")
  @@index([buId, createdAt, id], map: "mt_draft_bu_created_idx")
  @@map("message_template_drafts")
}

model MessageTemplateRevision {
  id String @id @default(uuid())
  draftId String @map("draft_id")
  draft MessageTemplateDraft @relation(fields: [draftId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade, map: "mt_revision_draft_fkey")
  revision Int
  definition Json
  createdAt DateTime @default(now()) @map("created_at")

  @@unique([draftId, revision], map: "mt_revision_draft_rev_uq")
  @@map("message_template_revisions")
}

enum MessageTemplateOperationPhase {
  PREPARED
  SENDING
  ACCEPTED
  REJECTED
  AMBIGUOUS
}

enum MessageTemplateAcceptanceProvenance {
  ACKNOWLEDGED
  CORRELATED_BY_DEFINITION
}

/// Durable, immutable submit intent. The provider request is always derived
/// from this snapshot and can never be rebound to a later credential/account.
model MessageTemplateOperation {
